FSMenu.prototype.activateMenu = function(id, subInd) {
    with (this) {
        if (!isDOM || !document.documentElement)
            return;
        var fsmFB = getRef('fsmenu-fallback');
        if (fsmFB) {
            fsmFB.rel = 'alternate stylesheet';
            fsmFB.disabled = true
        }
        var a, ul, li, parUL, mRoot = getRef(id), nodes;
        if (!FSMenu._count)
            FSMenu._count = 1;
        var evtProp = navigator.userAgent.indexOf('Safari') > -1 || isOp ? 'safRtnVal' : 'returnValue';
        var lists = mRoot.getElementsByTagName('ul');
        for (var i = 0; i < lists.length; i++) {
            li = ul = lists[i];
            while (li) {
                if (li.nodeName.toLowerCase() == 'li')
                    break;
                li = li.parentNode
            }
            if (!li)
                continue;
            parUL = li;
            while (parUL) {
                if (parUL.nodeName.toLowerCase() == 'ul')
                    break;
                parUL = parUL.parentNode
            }
            a = li.getElementsByTagName('a');
            if (!a)
                continue;
            a = a.item(0);
            var menuID;
            if (ul.id)
                menuID = ul.id;
            else {
                menuID = myName + '-id-' + FSMenu._count++;
                ul.setAttribute('id', menuID)
            }
            if (menus[menuID] && menus[menuID].lyr.ref == ul)
                continue;
            menus[menuID] = new FSMenuNode(menuID,false,this);
            var rootItem = (li.parentNode == mRoot) ? 1 : 0;
            var eShow = new Function('with(' + myName + '){var m=menus["' + menuID + '"],pM=menus["' + parUL.id + '"];if(!showOnClick||(showOnClick==1&&!' + rootItem + ')||((showOnClick<=2)&&((pM&&pM.child)||(m&&m.visible))))show("' + menuID + '",this)}');
            var eHide = new Function('e','if(e.' + evtProp + '!=false)' + myName + '.hide("' + menuID + '")');
            addEvent(a, 'mouseover', eShow);
            addEvent(a, 'mouseout', eHide);
            addEvent(a, 'focus', eShow);
            addEvent(a, 'blur', eHide);
            addEvent(a, 'click', new Function('e','var s=' + myName + '.showOnClick,m=' + myName + '.menus["' + menuID + '"];if(!((s==1&&' + rootItem + ')||s>=2))return;' + myName + '[m&&m.visible?"hide":"show"]("' + menuID + '",this);if(e.cancelable&&e.preventDefault)e.preventDefault();e.' + evtProp + '=false;return false'));
            if (subInd)
                a.insertBefore(subInd.cloneNode(true), a.firstChild)
        }
        var aNodes = mRoot.getElementsByTagName('a');
        for (var i = 0; i < aNodes.length; i++) {
            addEvent(aNodes[i], 'focus', new Function('e','var node=this.parentNode;while(node){if(node.onfocus)node.onfocus(e);node=node.parentNode}'));
            addEvent(aNodes[i], 'blur', new Function('e','var node=this.parentNode;while(node){if(node.onblur)node.onblur(e);node=node.parentNode}'))
        }
        if (hideOnClick)
            addEvent(mRoot, 'click', new Function('e','if(e.' + evtProp + '!=false)' + myName + '.hideAll()'));
        menus[id] = new FSMenuNode(id,true,this)
    }
}

var page = {
    win: self,
    minW: 0,
    minH: 0,
    MS: isIE && !isOp,
    db: document.compatMode && document.compatMode.indexOf('CSS') > -1 ? 'documentElement' : 'body'

page.elmPos = function(e, p) {
    var x = 0
      , y = 0
      , w = p ? p : this.win;
    e = e ? (e.substr ? (isNS4 ? w.document.anchors[e] : getRef(e, w)) : e) : p;
    if (isNS4) {
        if (e && (e != p)) {
            x = e.x;
            y = e.y
        }
        ;if (p) {
            x += p.pageX;
            y += p.pageY
        }
    }
    if (e && this.MS && navigator.platform.indexOf('Mac') > -1 && e.tagName == 'A') {
        e.onfocus = new Function('with(event){self.tmpX=clientX-offsetX;self.tmpY=clientY-offsetY}');
        e.focus();
        x = tmpX;
        y = tmpY;
        e.blur()
    } else
        while (e) {
            x += e.offsetLeft;
            y += e.offsetTop;
            e = e.offsetParent
        }
    return {
        x: x,
        y: y
    }
}

    }

    }

    pmDesignator: "p.m.",

    twoDigitYearMax: 2029,
    dateElementOrder: "dmy",
    formatPatterns: {
        shortDate: "d/MM/yyyy",
        longDate: "dddd, d MMMM yyyy",
        shortTime: "h:mm tt",
        longTime: "h:mm:ss tt",
        fullDateTime: "dddd, d MMMM yyyy h:mm:ss tt",
        sortableDateTime: "yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss",
        universalSortableDateTime: "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ssZ",
        rfc1123: "ddd, dd MMM yyyy HH:mm:ss GMT",
        monthDay: "dd MMMM",
        yearMonth: "MMMM yyyy"
    },
    regexPatterns: {
        jan: /^jan(uary)?/i,
        feb: /^feb(ruary)?/i,
        mar: /^mar(ch)?/i,
        apr: /^apr(il)?/i,
        may: /^may/i,
        jun: /^jun(e)?/i,
        jul: /^jul(y)?/i,
        aug: /^aug(ust)?/i,
        sep: /^sep(t(ember)?)?/i,
        oct: /^oct(ober)?/i,
        nov: /^nov(ember)?/i,
        dec: /^dec(ember)?/i,
        sun: /^su(n(day)?)?/i,
        mon: /^mo(n(day)?)?/i,
        tue: /^tu(e(s(day)?)?)?/i,
        wed: /^we(d(nesday)?)?/i,
        thu: /^th(u(r(s(day)?)?)?)?/i,
        fri: /^fr(i(day)?)?/i,
        sat: /^sa(t(urday)?)?/i,
        future: /^next/i,
        past: /^last|past|prev(ious)?/i,
        add: /^(\+|after|from)/i,
        subtract: /^(\-|before|ago)/i,
        yesterday: /^yesterday/i,
        today: /^t(oday)?/i,
        tomorrow: /^tomorrow/i,
        now: /^n(ow)?/i,
        millisecond: /^ms|milli(second)?s?/i,
        second: /^sec(ond)?s?/i,
        minute: /^min(ute)?s?/i,
        hour: /^h(ou)?rs?/i,
        week: /^w(ee)?k/i,
        month: /^m(o(nth)?s?)?/i,
        day: /^d(ays?)?/i,
        year: /^y((ea)?rs?)?/i,
        shortMeridian: /^(a|p)/i,
        longMeridian: /^(a\.?m?\.?|p\.?m?\.?)/i,
        timezone: /^((e(s|d)t|c(s|d)t|m(s|d)t|p(s|d)t)|((gmt)?\s*(\+|\-)\s*\d\d\d\d?)|gmt)/i,
        ordinalSuffix: /^\s*(st|nd|rd|th)/i,
        timeContext: /^\s*(\:|a|p)/i
    },
    abbreviatedTimeZoneStandard: {
        GMT: "-000",
        EST: "-0400",
        CST: "-0500",
        MST: "-0600",
        PST: "-0700"
    },
    abbreviatedTimeZoneDST: {
        GMT: "-000",
        EDT: "-0500",
        CDT: "-0600",
        MDT: "-0700",
        PDT: "-0800"
    }
};
Date.getMonthNumberFromName = function(name) {
    var n = Date.CultureInfo.monthNames
      , m = Date.CultureInfo.abbreviatedMonthNames
      , s = name.toLowerCase();
    for (var i = 0; i < n.length; i++) {
        if (n[i].toLowerCase() == s || m[i].toLowerCase() == s) {
            return i;
        }
    }
    return -1;
}
;
Date.getDayNumberFromName = function(name) {
    var n = Date.CultureInfo.dayNames
      , m = Date.CultureInfo.abbreviatedDayNames
      , o = Date.CultureInfo.shortestDayNames
      , s = name.toLowerCase();
    for (var i = 0; i < n.length; i++) {
        if (n[i].toLowerCase() == s || m[i].toLowerCase() == s) {
            return i;
        }
    }
    return -1;
}
;
Date.isLeapYear = function(year) {
    return (((year % 4 === 0) && (year % 100 !== 0)) || (year % 400 === 0));
}
;
Date.getDaysInMonth = function(year, month) {
    return [31, (Date.isLeapYear(year) ? 29 : 28), 31, 30, 31, 30, 31, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31][month];
}
;
Date.getTimezoneOffset = function(s, dst) {
    return (dst || false) ? Date.CultureInfo.abbreviatedTimeZoneDST[s.toUpperCase()] : Date.CultureInfo.abbreviatedTimeZoneStandard[s.toUpperCase()];
}
;
Date.getTimezoneAbbreviation = function(offset, dst) {
    var n = (dst || false) ? Date.CultureInfo.abbreviatedTimeZoneDST : Date.CultureInfo.abbreviatedTimeZoneStandard, p;
    for (p in n) {
        if (n[p] === offset) {
            return p;
        }
    }
    return null;
}
;
Date.prototype.clone = function() {
    return new Date(this.getTime());
}
;
Date.prototype.compareTo = function(date) {
    if (isNaN(this)) {
        throw new Error(this);
    }
    if (date instanceof Date && !isNaN(date)) {
        return (this > date) ? 1 : (this < date) ? -1 : 0;
    } else {
        throw new TypeError(date);
    }
}
;
Date.prototype.equals = function(date) {
    return (this.compareTo(date) === 0);
}
;
Date.prototype.between = function(start, end) {
    var t = this.getTime();
    return t >= start.getTime() && t <= end.getTime();
}
;
Date.prototype.addMilliseconds = function(value) {
    this.setMilliseconds(this.getMilliseconds() + value);
    return this;
}
;
Date.prototype.addSeconds = function(value) {
    return this.addMilliseconds(value * 1000);
}
;
Date.prototype.addMinutes = function(value) {
    return this.addMilliseconds(value * 60000);
}
;
Date.prototype.addHours = function(value) {
    return this.addMilliseconds(value * 3600000);
}
;
Date.prototype.addDays = function(value) {
    return this.addMilliseconds(value * 86400000);
}
;
Date.prototype.addWeeks = function(value) {
    return this.addMilliseconds(value * 604800000);
}
;
Date.prototype.addMonths = function(value) {
    var n = this.getDate();
    this.setDate(1);
    this.setMonth(this.getMonth() + value);
    this.setDate(Math.min(n, this.getDaysInMonth()));
    return this;
}
;
Date.prototype.addYears = function(value) {
    return this.addMonths(value * 12);
}
;
Date.prototype.add = function(config) {
    if (typeof config == "number") {
        this._orient = config;
        return this;
    }
    var x = config;
    if (x.millisecond || x.milliseconds) {
        this.addMilliseconds(x.millisecond || x.milliseconds);
    }
    if (x.second || x.seconds) {
        this.addSeconds(x.second || x.seconds);
    }
    if (x.minute || x.minutes) {
        this.addMinutes(x.minute || x.minutes);
    }
    if (x.hour || x.hours) {
        this.addHours(x.hour || x.hours);
    }
    if (x.month || x.months) {
        this.addMonths(x.month || x.months);
    }
    if (x.year || x.years) {
        this.addYears(x.year || x.years);
    }
    if (x.day || x.days) {
        this.addDays(x.day || x.days);
    }
    return this;
}
;
Date._validate = function(value, min, max, name) {
    if (typeof value != "number") {
        throw new TypeError(value + " is not a Number.");
    } else if (value < min || value > max) {
        throw new RangeError(value + " is not a valid value for " + name + ".");
    }
    return true;
}
;
Date.validateMillisecond = function(n) {
    return Date._validate(n, 0, 999, "milliseconds");
}
;
Date.validateSecond = function(n) {
    return Date._validate(n, 0, 59, "seconds");
}
;
Date.validateMinute = function(n) {
    return Date._validate(n, 0, 59, "minutes");
}
;
Date.validateHour = function(n) {
    return Date._validate(n, 0, 23, "hours");
}
;
Date.validateDay = function(n, year, month) {
    return Date._validate(n, 1, Date.getDaysInMonth(year, month), "days");
}
;
Date.validateMonth = function(n) {
    return Date._validate(n, 0, 11, "months");
}
;
Date.validateYear = function(n) {
    return Date._validate(n, 1, 9999, "seconds");
}
;
Date.prototype.set = function(config) {
    var x = config;
    if (!x.millisecond && x.millisecond !== 0) {
        x.millisecond = -1;
    }
    if (!x.second && x.second !== 0) {
        x.second = -1;
    }
    if (!x.minute && x.minute !== 0) {
        x.minute = -1;
    }
    if (!x.hour && x.hour !== 0) {
        x.hour = -1;
    }
    if (!x.day && x.day !== 0) {
        x.day = -1;
    }
    if (!x.month && x.month !== 0) {
        x.month = -1;
    }
    if (!x.year && x.year !== 0) {
        x.year = -1;
    }
    if (x.millisecond != -1 && Date.validateMillisecond(x.millisecond)) {
        this.addMilliseconds(x.millisecond - this.getMilliseconds());
    }
    if (x.second != -1 && Date.validateSecond(x.second)) {
        this.addSeconds(x.second - this.getSeconds());
    }
    if (x.minute != -1 && Date.validateMinute(x.minute)) {
        this.addMinutes(x.minute - this.getMinutes());
    }
    if (x.hour != -1 && Date.validateHour(x.hour)) {
        this.addHours(x.hour - this.getHours());
    }
    if (x.month !== -1 && Date.validateMonth(x.month)) {
        this.addMonths(x.month - this.getMonth());
    }
    if (x.year != -1 && Date.validateYear(x.year)) {
        this.addYears(x.year - this.getFullYear());
    }
    if (x.day != -1 && Date.validateDay(x.day, this.getFullYear(), this.getMonth())) {
        this.addDays(x.day - this.getDate());
    }
    if (x.timezone) {
        this.setTimezone(x.timezone);
    }
    if (x.timezoneOffset) {
        this.setTimezoneOffset(x.timezoneOffset);
    }
    return this;
}
;
Date.prototype.clearTime = function() {
    this.setHours(0);
    this.setMinutes(0);
    this.setSeconds(0);
    this.setMilliseconds(0);
    return this;
}
;
Date.prototype.isLeapYear = function() {
    var y = this.getFullYear();
    return (((y % 4 === 0) && (y % 100 !== 0)) || (y % 400 === 0));
}
;
Date.prototype.isWeekday = function() {
    return !(this.is().sat() || this.is().sun());
}
;
Date.prototype.getDaysInMonth = function() {
    return Date.getDaysInMonth(this.getFullYear(), this.getMonth());
}
;
Date.prototype.moveToFirstDayOfMonth = function() {
    return this.set({
        day: 1
    });
}
;
Date.prototype.moveToLastDayOfMonth = function() {
    return this.set({
        day: this.getDaysInMonth()
    });
}
;
Date.prototype.moveToDayOfWeek = function(day, orient) {
    var diff = (day - this.getDay() + 7 * (orient || +1)) % 7;
    return this.addDays((diff === 0) ? diff += 7 * (orient || +1) : diff);
}
;
Date.prototype.moveToMonth = function(month, orient) {
    var diff = (month - this.getMonth() + 12 * (orient || +1)) % 12;
    return this.addMonths((diff === 0) ? diff += 12 * (orient || +1) : diff);
}
;
Date.prototype.getDayOfYear = function() {
    return Math.floor((this - new Date(this.getFullYear(),0,1)) / 86400000);
}
;
Date.prototype.getWeekOfYear = function(firstDayOfWeek) {
    var y = this.getFullYear()
      , m = this.getMonth()
      , d = this.getDate();
    var dow = firstDayOfWeek || Date.CultureInfo.firstDayOfWeek;
    var offset = 7 + 1 - new Date(y,0,1).getDay();
    if (offset == 8) {
        offset = 1;
    }
    var daynum = ((Date.UTC(y, m, d, 0, 0, 0) - Date.UTC(y, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0)) / 86400000) + 1;
    var w = Math.floor((daynum - offset + 7) / 7);
    if (w === dow) {
        y--;
        var prevOffset = 7 + 1 - new Date(y,0,1).getDay();
        if (prevOffset == 2 || prevOffset == 8) {
            w = 53;
        } else {
            w = 52;
        }
    }
    return w;
}
;
Date.prototype.isDST = function() {
    console.log('isDST');
    return this.toString().match(/(E|C|M|P)(S|D)T/)[2] == "D";
}
;
Date.prototype.getTimezone = function() {
    return Date.getTimezoneAbbreviation(this.getUTCOffset, this.isDST());
}
;
Date.prototype.setTimezoneOffset = function(s) {
    var here = this.getTimezoneOffset()
      , there = Number(s) * -6 / 10;
    this.addMinutes(there - here);
    return this;
}
;
Date.prototype.setTimezone = function(s) {
    return this.setTimezoneOffset(Date.getTimezoneOffset(s));
}
;
Date.prototype.getUTCOffset = function() {
    var n = this.getTimezoneOffset() * -10 / 6, r;
    if (n < 0) {
        r = (n - 10000).toString();
        return r[0] + r.substr(2);
    } else {
        r = (n + 10000).toString();
        return "+" + r.substr(1);
    }
}
;
Date.prototype.getDayName = function(abbrev) {
    return abbrev ? Date.CultureInfo.abbreviatedDayNames[this.getDay()] : Date.CultureInfo.dayNames[this.getDay()];
}
;
Date.prototype.getMonthName = function(abbrev) {
    return abbrev ? Date.CultureInfo.abbreviatedMonthNames[this.getMonth()] : Date.CultureInfo.monthNames[this.getMonth()];
}
;
Date.prototype._toString = Date.prototype.toString;
Date.prototype.toString = function(format) {
    var self = this;
    var p = function p(s) {
        return (s.toString().length == 1) ? "0" + s : s;
    };
    return format ? format.replace(/dd?d?d?|MM?M?M?|yy?y?y?|hh?|HH?|mm?|ss?|tt?|zz?z?/g, function(format) {
        switch (format) {
        case "hh":
            return p(self.getHours() < 13 ? self.getHours() : (self.getHours() - 12));
        case "h":
            return self.getHours() < 13 ? self.getHours() : (self.getHours() - 12);
        case "HH":
            return p(self.getHours());
        case "H":
            return self.getHours();
        case "mm":
            return p(self.getMinutes());
        case "m":
            return self.getMinutes();
        case "ss":
            return p(self.getSeconds());
        case "s":
            return self.getSeconds();
        case "yyyy":
            return self.getFullYear();
        case "yy":
            return self.getFullYear().toString().substring(2, 4);
        case "dddd":
            return self.getDayName();
        case "ddd":
            return self.getDayName(true);
        case "dd":
            return p(self.getDate());
        case "d":
            return self.getDate().toString();
        case "MMMM":
            return self.getMonthName();
        case "MMM":
            return self.getMonthName(true);
        case "MM":
            return p((self.getMonth() + 1));
        case "M":
            return self.getMonth() + 1;
        case "t":
            return self.getHours() < 12 ? Date.CultureInfo.amDesignator.substring(0, 1) : Date.CultureInfo.pmDesignator.substring(0, 1);
        case "tt":
            return self.getHours() < 12 ? Date.CultureInfo.amDesignator : Date.CultureInfo.pmDesignator;
        case "zzz":
        case "zz":
        case "z":
            return "";
        }
    }) : this._toString();
}
;
Date.now = function() {
    return new Date();
}
;
Date.today = function() {
    return Date.now().clearTime();
}
;
Date.prototype._orient = +1;
Date.prototype.next = function() {
    this._orient = +1;
    return this;
}
;
Date.prototype.last = Date.prototype.prev = Date.prototype.previous = function() {
    this._orient = -1;
    return this;
}
;
Date.prototype._is = false;
Date.prototype.is = function() {
    this._is = true;
    return this;
}
;
Number.prototype._dateElement = "day";
Number.prototype.fromNow = function() {
    var c = {};
    c[this._dateElement] = this;
    return Date.now().add(c);
}
;
Number.prototype.ago = function() {
    var c = {};
    c[this._dateElement] = this * -1;
    return Date.now().add(c);
}
;
(function() {
    var $D = Date.prototype
      , $N = Number.prototype;
    var dx = ("sunday monday tuesday wednesday thursday friday saturday").split(/\s/), mx = ("january february march april may june july august september october november december").split(/\s/), px = ("Millisecond Second Minute Hour Day Week Month Year").split(/\s/), de;
    var df = function(n) {
        return function() {
            if (this._is) {
                this._is = false;
                return this.getDay() == n;
            }
            return this.moveToDayOfWeek(n, this._orient);
        }
        ;
    };
    for (var i = 0; i < dx.length; i++) {
        $D[dx[i]] = $D[dx[i].substring(0, 3)] = df(i);
    }
    var mf = function(n) {
        return function() {
            if (this._is) {
                this._is = false;
                return this.getMonth() === n;
            }
            return this.moveToMonth(n, this._orient);
        }
        ;
    };
    for (var j = 0; j < mx.length; j++) {
        $D[mx[j]] = $D[mx[j].substring(0, 3)] = mf(j);
    }
    var ef = function(j) {
        return function() {
            if (j.substring(j.length - 1) != "s") {
                j += "s";
            }
            return this["add" + j](this._orient);
        }
        ;
    };
    var nf = function(n) {
        return function() {
            this._dateElement = n;
            return this;
        }
        ;
    };
    for (var k = 0; k < px.length; k++) {
        de = px[k].toLowerCase();
        $D[de] = $D[de + "s"] = ef(px[k]);
        $N[de] = $N[de + "s"] = nf(de);
    }
}());
Date.prototype.toJSONString = function() {
    return this.toString("yyyy-MM-ddThh:mm:ssZ");
}
;
Date.prototype.toShortDateString = function() {
    return this.toString(Date.CultureInfo.formatPatterns.shortDatePattern);
}

Date.prototype.toLongDateString = function() {
    return this.toString(Date.CultureInfo.formatPatterns.longDatePattern);
}

    return this.toString(Date.CultureInfo.formatPatterns.shortTimePattern);

;
Date.prototype.toLongTimeString = function() {
    return this.toString(Date.CultureInfo.formatPatterns.longTimePattern);

Date.prototype.getOrdinal = function() {
    switch (this.getDate()) {
    case 1:
    case 21:
    case 31:
        return "st";
    case 2:
    case 22:
        return "nd";
    case 3:
    case 23:
        return "rd";
    default:
        return "th";
    }
}

(function() {
    Date.Parsing = {
        Exception: function(s) {
            this.message = "Parse error at '" + s.substring(0, 10) + " ...'";
        }
    };
    var $P = Date.Parsing;
    var _ = $P.Operators = {
        rtoken: function(r) {
            return function(s) {
                var mx = s.match(r);
                if (mx) {
                    return ([mx[0], s.substring(mx[0].length)]);
                } else {
                    throw new $P.Exception(s);
                }
            }
            ;
        },
        token: function(s) {
            return function(s) {
                return _.rtoken(new RegExp("^\s*" + s + "\s*"))(s);
            }
            ;
        },
        stoken: function(s) {
            return _.rtoken(new RegExp("^" + s));
        },
        until: function(p) {
            return function(s) {
                var qx = []
                  , rx = null;
                while (s.length) {
                    try {
                        rx = p.call(this, s);
                    } catch (e) {
                        qx.push(rx[0]);
                        s = rx[1];
                        continue;
                    }
                    break;
                }
                return [qx, s];
            }
            ;
        },
        many: function(p) {
            return function(s) {
                var rx = []
                  , r = null;
                while (s.length) {
                    try {
                        r = p.call(this, s);
                    } catch (e) {
                        return [rx, s];
                    }
                    rx.push(r[0]);
                    s = r[1];
                }
                return [rx, s];
            }
            ;
        },
        optional: function(p) {
            return function(s) {
                var r = null;
                try {
                    r = p.call(this, s);
                } catch (e) {
                    return [null, s];
                }
                return [r[0], r[1]];
            }
            ;
        },
        not: function(p) {
            return function(s) {
                try {
                    p.call(this, s);
                } catch (e) {
                    return [null, s];
                }
                throw new $P.Exception(s);
            }
            ;
        },
        ignore: function(p) {
            return p ? function(s) {
                var r = null;
                r = p.call(this, s);
                return [null, r[1]];
            }
            : null;
        },
        product: function() {
            var px = arguments[0]
              , qx = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments, 1)
              , rx = [];
            for (var i = 0; i < px.length; i++) {
                rx.push(_.each(px[i], qx));
            }
            return rx;
        },
        cache: function(rule) {
            var cache = {}
              , r = null;
            return function(s) {
                try {
                    r = cache[s] = (cache[s] || rule.call(this, s));
                } catch (e) {
                    r = cache[s] = e;
                }
                if (r instanceof $P.Exception) {
                    throw r;
                } else {
                    return r;
                }
            }
            ;
        },
        any: function() {
            var px = arguments;
            return function(s) {
                var r = null;
                for (var i = 0; i < px.length; i++) {
                    if (px[i] == null) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    try {
                        r = (px[i].call(this, s));
                    } catch (e) {
                        r = null;
                    }
                    if (r) {
                        return r;
                    }
                }
                throw new $P.Exception(s);
            }
            ;
        },
        each: function() {
            var px = arguments;
            return function(s) {
                var rx = []
                  , r = null;
                for (var i = 0; i < px.length; i++) {
                    if (px[i] == null) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    try {
                        r = (px[i].call(this, s));
                    } catch (e) {
                        throw new $P.Exception(s);
                    }
                    rx.push(r[0]);
                    s = r[1];
                }
                return [rx, s];
            }
            ;
        },
        all: function() {
            var px = arguments
              , _ = _;
            return _.each(_.optional(px));
        },
        sequence: function(px, d, c) {
            d = d || _.rtoken(/^\s*/);
            c = c || null;
            if (px.length == 1) {
                return px[0];
            }
            return function(s) {
                var r = null
                  , q = null;
                var rx = [];
                for (var i = 0; i < px.length; i++) {
                    try {
                        r = px[i].call(this, s);
                    } catch (e) {
                        break;
                    }
                    rx.push(r[0]);
                    try {
                        q = d.call(this, r[1]);
                    } catch (ex) {
                        q = null;
                        break;
                    }
                    s = q[1];

            }]
        });
        $jDialogEl.dialog("option", "width", dialogWidth);
    }
    function _confirm(title, message, externalYesHandler, externalNoHandler, invokeSiblingMask, yesLabel, noLabel, showWarningIcon) {
        if (_uiLibrary == "yui") {
            hlk.util.DialogYesNo.showYesNoDialog(message, title, externalYesHandler, externalNoHandler, invokeSiblingMask, yesLabel, noLabel, showWarningIcon);
        } else {
            _confirmJquery(title, message, externalYesHandler, externalNoHandler, invokeSiblingMask, yesLabel, noLabel, showWarningIcon);
        }
    }
    function _confirmJquery(title, message, externalYesHandler, externalNoHandler, invokeSiblingMask, yesLabel, noLabel, showWarningIcon) {
        _initDialogEl();
        var dialogTitle = title;
        if (dialogTitle == null) {
            dialogTitle = "Please confirm";
        }
        var dialogContent = message;
        if (showWarningIcon == true) {
            dialogContent = "<div class=\"ui-icon ui-icon-alert\" style=\"margin:0 7px 5px 0;\"></div>" + dialogContent;
        }
        var $jDialogEl = jQuery("#" + dialogElId);
        $jDialogEl.html(dialogContent);
        $jDialogEl.dialog({
            dialogClass: "no-close",
            title: dialogTitle,
            minWidth: 400,
            resizable: false,
            modal: true,
            open: function(event, ui) {
                if (invokeSiblingMask != false) {
                    var parentWindow = window.parent;
                    for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
                        if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].mask == "function") {
                            parentWindow.frames[i].mask();
                        }
                    }
                }
                $j(window).on("keydown.dialog", function(event) {
                    if (event.which == 8) {
                        event.preventDefault();
                    }
                });
            },
            close: function(event, ui) {
                if (invokeSiblingMask != false) {
                    var parentWindow = window.parent;
                    for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
                        if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].unmask == "function") {
                            parentWindow.frames[i].unmask();
                        }
                    }
                }
                $j(window).off("keydown.dialog");
            },
            buttons: [{
                text: yesLabel,
                click: function() {
                    if (externalYesHandler != null) {
                        externalYesHandler();
                    }
                    jQuery(this).dialog("close");
                }
            }, {
                text: noLabel,
                click: function() {
                    if (externalNoHandler != null) {
                        externalNoHandler();
                    }
                    jQuery(this).dialog("close");
                }
            }]
        });
    }
    return {
        init: _init,
        alert: _alert,
        confirm: _confirm,
        showWarning: _showWarning
    };
}

hlk.util.DialogYesNo = {
    yesNoDialog: null,
    externalYesHandler: null,
    externalNoHandler: null,
    showYesNoDialog: function(message, title, externalYesHandler, externalNoHandler, invokeSiblingMask, yesLabel, noLabel, showWarningIcon) {
        if (this.yesNoDialog == null) {
            this.yesNoDialog = new YAHOO.widget.SimpleDialog("yesNoDialog",{
                width: "400px",
                fixedcenter: true,
                visible: false,
                modal: true,
                close: false,
                constraintoviewport: true,
                buttons: [{
                    text: yesLabel == null ? "Yes" : yesLabel,
                    handler: hlk.util.DialogYesNo.handleYes,
                    isDefault: true
                }, {
                    text: noLabel == null ? "No" : noLabel,
                    handler: hlk.util.DialogYesNo.handleNo
                }]
            });
            this.yesNoDialog.showEvent.subscribe(function() {
                jQuery(window).on("keydown.dialogYesNo", function(event) {
                    if (event.which == 8) {
                        event.preventDefault();
                    }
                });
            });
            this.yesNoDialog.hideEvent.subscribe(function() {
                jQuery(window).off("keydown.dialogYesNo");
            });
        }
        if (title == null) {
            title = "Please confirm";
        }
        this.yesNoDialog.setHeader("<span class='warningHeader'>" + title + "</span>");
        this.yesNoDialog.setBody("<br><br><span class='warningContainer'>" + message + "</span>");
        if (showWarningIcon == null || showWarningIcon == true) {
            this.yesNoDialog.cfg.setProperty("icon", YAHOO.widget.SimpleDialog.ICON_WARN);
        }
        this.yesNoDialog.render(document.body);
        this.yesNoDialog.show();
        if (externalYesHandler != null) {
            this.externalYesHandler = externalYesHandler;
        }
        if (externalNoHandler != null) {
            this.externalNoHandler = externalNoHandler;
        }
        if (invokeSiblingMask != false) {
            var parentWindow = window.parent;
            for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
                if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].mask == "function") {
                    parentWindow.frames[i].mask();
                }
            }
        }
    },
    handleNo: function(invokeSiblingMask) {
        hlk.util.DialogYesNo.yesNoDialog.hide();
        if (invokeSiblingMask != false) {
            var parentWindow = window.parent;
            for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
                if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].unmask == "function") {
                    parentWindow.frames[i].unmask();
                }
            }
        }
        if (hlk.util.DialogYesNo.externalNoHandler != null) {
            hlk.util.DialogYesNo.externalNoHandler();
        }
    },
    handleYes: function(invokeSiblingMask) {
        hlk.util.DialogYesNo.yesNoDialog.hide();
        if (invokeSiblingMask != false) {
            var parentWindow = window.parent;
            for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
                if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].unmask == "function") {
                    parentWindow.frames[i].unmask();
                }
            }
        }
        if (hlk.util.DialogYesNo.externalYesHandler != null) {
            hlk.util.DialogYesNo.externalYesHandler();
        }
    }

hlk.util.DhtmlUtil = {
    getElementValue: function(nameOrHtmlElement) {
        var elements = null;
        if (jQuery.type(nameOrHtmlElement) === "string") {
            elements = jQuery(document.getElementsByName(nameOrHtmlElement));
        } else {
            elements = jQuery(nameOrHtmlElement);
        }
        var elementType = elements.prop("type");
        switch (elementType) {
        case "checkbox":
        case "radio":
            for (var i = 0; i < elements.length; i++) {
                if (elements[i].checked) {
                    return elements[i].value;
                }
            }
            return null;
            break;
        default:
            return elements.val();
        }
    },
    showHideElement: function(element, displayValue) {
        if (element != null) {
            element.style.display = displayValue;
        }
    },
    toggleElementsByCheckboxChecked: function(checkbox, elementIds) {
        var checkboxEl = checkbox;
        if (typeof (checkbox) == "string") {
            checkboxEl = document.getElementById(checkbox);
        }
        if (checkboxEl != null) {
            for (var i = 1; i < arguments.length; i++) {
                var elementId = arguments[i];
                if (typeof (elementId) != 'undefined' && elementId != null) {
                    var element = document.getElementById(elementId);
                    if (element != null) {
                        if (checkboxEl.checked) {
                            element.style.display = "block";
                        } else {
                            element.style.display = "none";
                        }
                    } else {
                        alert(elementId + " not found!");
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    },
    clearChildValues: function(parentId) {
        var jParentElement = jQuery("#" + parentId.replace(/\./g, "\\."));
        hlk.util.DhtmlUtil.clearJQueryElementChildValues(jParentElement);
    },
    clearJQueryElementChildValues: function(jQueryElement) {
        var parentElement = jQueryElement;
        parentElement.find("input:text:not(.notClearable)").val("");
        parentElement.find("input:password:not(.notClearable)").val("");
        parentElement.find("input[type='hidden']:not(.notClearable)").val("");
        parentElement.find("select:not(.notClearable)").val("");
        parentElement.find("textarea:not(.notClearable)").val("");
        var checkedElements = parentElement.find("input:checked:not(.notClearable)");
        for (var i = 0; i < checkedElements.length; i++) {
            checkedElements[i].checked = false;
        }
        var contentEditable = parentElement.find("div[contentEditable='true']");
        for (var i = 0; i < contentEditable.length; i++) {
            contentEditable[i].innerHTML = "";
        }
    },
    clearChildClass: function(parentId, className) {
        var parentElement = jQuery("#" + parentId.replace(".", "\\."));
        jQuery(parentElement).find("." + className).removeClass(className);
    },
    getTopWindow: function(targetWindow) {
        var topWindow = targetWindow.parent;
        if (topWindow == targetWindow) {
            return topWindow;
        } else {
            return this.getTopWindow(topWindow);
        }
    },
    isIE6: function() {
        return jQuery.browser.msie && jQuery.browser.version == "6.0";
    },
    getNamePrefixIfHasIndex: function(element) {
        var regex = new RegExp("^(.+?)\\[\\d*\\]","g");
        var elemNamePrefix = regex.exec(jQuery(element).attr("name"));
        if (elemNamePrefix != null) {
            return elemNamePrefix[1];
        }
        return null;
    },
    setIFrameContent: function(document, iframeId, content) {
        var iframe = document.getElementById(iframeId);
        if (iframe != null) {
            var doc = null;
            if (iframe.contentWindow) {
                doc = iframe.contentWindow.document;
            } else if (iframe.contentDocument) {
                doc = iframe.contentDocument;
            }
            if (doc == null) {
                alert("Unable to set the content of the iframe " + iframeId);
            } else {
                doc.open();
                doc.write(content);
                doc.close();
            }
        } else {
            alert("IFrame (" + iframeId + ") not found.");
        }
    }
};

hlk.util.StringUtil = {
    getSubstringAfterLast: function(text, delimiter) {
        if (text == null || delimiter == null || text.indexOf(delimiter) == -1) {
            return null;
        }
        var textArray = text.split(delimiter);
        return textArray[textArray.length - 1];
    },
    getSubstringBeforeLast: function(text, delimiter) {
        if (text == null || delimiter == null || text.indexOf(delimiter) == -1) {
            return null;
        }
        return text.substring(0, text.lastIndexOf(delimiter));
    }
}

hlk.util.YuiExtension = {
    mimeTypeToLabelMap: {
        "text/plain": "txt",
        "application/msword": "doc",
        "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document": "docx"
    },
    parseDate: function(oData) {
        return Date.parse(oData);
    },
    unescapeWhiteSpace: function(value) {
        var re = /(&nbsp;)/g;
        var newValue = value.replace(re, " ");
        return newValue;
    },
    escapeHtml: function(oData) {
        var oDataString = new String(oData);
        return oDataString.escapeHTML();
    },
    unescapeHtml: function(oData) {
        var oDataString = new String(oData);
        return oDataString.unescapeHTML();
    }
};
YAHOO.widget.DataTable.Formatter["hlk.checkbox"] = function(el, oRecord, oColumn, oData) {
    var bChecked = "";
    var disabled = "";
    var fieldName = "yui2.hlk[N].checkbox";
    if (oData == "disabled") {
        bChecked = "";
        disabled = "disabled";
    } else if (oData == "hidden") {
        bChecked = "";
        disabled = "hidden";
    } else {
        bChecked = oData;
    }
    bChecked = (bChecked) ? " checked=\"checked\" " : "";
    fieldName = " name=\"" + (typeof (oColumn.name) === "undefined" || oColumn.name == "" ? fieldName : oColumn.name).replace(/(^.+?\[)N(\])/g, "$1" + this.getRecordIndex(oRecord) + "$2") + "\" ";
    if (disabled != "hidden") {
        el.innerHTML = "<input type=\"checkbox\"" + bChecked + disabled + fieldName + " class=\"" + YAHOO.widget.DataTable.CLASS_CHECKBOX + "\" />";
    }
}
;
YAHOO.widget.DataTable.Formatter["hlk.radio"] = function(el, oRecord, oColumn, oData) {
    var bChecked = oData;
    bChecked = (bChecked) ? " checked=\"checked\"" : "";
    el.innerHTML = "<input type=\"radio\"" + bChecked + " name=\"" + "col-" + oColumn.getSanitizedKey() + "\"" + " class=\"" + YAHOO.widget.DataTable.CLASS_RADIO + "\" />";
}
;
YAHOO.widget.DataTable.Formatter["hlk.fileType"] = function(el, oRecord, oColumn, oData) {
    var oFileType = oData;
    if (oData != null) {
        oFileType = hlk.util.YuiExtension.mimeTypeToLabelMap[oData];
        if (oFileType == null || oFileType == "") {
            var indexOfSlash = oData.indexOf("/");
            if (indexOfSlash > 0) {
                oFileType = oData.substring(indexOfSlash + 1);
            }
            if (oFileType != null) {
                oFileType = oFileType.toLowerCase();
            }
        }
    }
    el.innerHTML = oFileType;
}
;
YAHOO.widget.DataTable.Formatter["hlk.date"] = function(el, oRecord, oColumn, oData) {
    var oDate = oData;
    el.innerHTML = hlk.util.DateUtil.convertDateToDDMMYYYY(oDate);
}
;
YAHOO.widget.DataTable.Formatter["hlk.dateWithTime"] = function(el, oRecord, oColumn, oData) {
    var oDate = oData;
    el.innerHTML = hlk.util.DateUtil.convertDateToDDMMYYYYWithTime(oDate);
}

YAHOO.widget.DataTable.Formatter["hlk.dateNaNToEmpty"] = function(el, oRecord, oColumn, oData) {

    el.innerHTML = hlk.util.DateUtil.convertDateToDDMMYYYY(oDate, true);
}

YAHOO.widget.DataTable.Formatter["hlk.escapeHtml"] = function(el, oRecord, oColumn, oData) {

function getObjectClass(obj) {
    if (obj && obj.constructor && obj.constructor.toString) {
        var arr = obj.constructor.toString().match(/function\s*(\w+)/);
        if (arr && arr.length == 2) {
            return arr[1];
        }
    }
    return undefined;
}
function getAttributeValue(el, name) {
    var result = null;
    name = name.toLowerCase();
    for (var x = 0; x < el.attributes.length; x++) {
        if (el.attributes[x].nodeName.toLowerCase() == name) {
            result = el.attributes[x].nodeValue;
            break;
        }
    }
    return (result);
}
function SelectionDataTableRender() {
    this.ds = new YAHOO.util.DataSource(YAHOO.util.Dom.get(this.htmlTable));
    this.ds.responseType = YAHOO.util.DataSource.TYPE_HTMLTABLE;
    this.ds.responseSchema = this.fieldDef;
    var skinCode = this.skinCode;
    var controlId;
    switch (this.type) {
    case "adjustable":
        controlId = this.htmlTable + "_addRowImage";
        this.columnDef.push({
            key: 'delete',
            label: ' ',
            formatter: function(elCell) {
                elCell.innerHTML = "<img src='skins/" + skinCode + "/images/delete.png' title='Delete Row' height='16' width='16'/>";
                elCell.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            },
            label: "<img id='" + controlId + "' src='skins/" + skinCode + "/images/add.png' title='Add Row' height='16' width='16'/>"
        });
        break;
    case "selectable":
        for (i in this.columnDef) {
            if (this.columnDef[i].key == 'selected') {
                controlId = this.htmlTable + "_selectAllCheckBox";
                this.columnDef[i].label = "<input type='checkbox' id='" + controlId + "'/>";
            }
        }
        break;
    }
    var dtProperties = {
        caption: ""
    };
    if (this.height != null) {
        dtProperties.height = this.height;
    }
    if (this.width != null) {
        dtProperties.width = this.width;
    }
    if (this.sortedBy != null) {
        dtProperties.sortedBy = this.sortedBy;
    }
    dtProperties.dateOptions = {
        format: '%d/%m/%Y'
    };
    if (this.scrolling)
        this.dt = new YAHOO.widget.ScrollingDataTable(this.container,this.columnDef,this.ds,dtProperties);
    else
        this.dt = new YAHOO.widget.DataTable(this.container,this.columnDef,this.ds,dtProperties);
    this.dt.subscribe('cellClickEvent', function(ev, obj) {
        var target = YAHOO.util.Event.getTarget(ev);
        var column = obj.dt.getColumn(target);
        if (column.key == 'delete') {
            if (!obj.confirmDelete) {
                deleteRow();
            } else {
                var message = "Are you sure you want to delete this record?";
                var title = "Please confirm";
                hlk.util.Dialog.confirm(title, message, deleteRow, null, true, "Yes", "No", true);
            }
        } else {
            if (obj.allowEditing) {
                obj.dt.onEventShowCellEditor(ev);
            }
        }
        function deleteRow() {
            obj.dt.deleteRow(target);
            if (obj.refreshSectionSummary) {
                obj.refreshSectionSummary();
            }
        }
    }, this);
    if (this.allowEditing) {
        var highlightEditableCell = function(oArgs) {
            var elCell = oArgs.target;
            if (YAHOO.util.Dom.hasClass(elCell, "yui-dt-editable")) {
                this.highlightCell(elCell);
            }
        };
        this.dt.subscribe("cellMouseoverEvent", highlightEditableCell);
        this.dt.subscribe("cellMouseoutEvent", this.dt.onEventUnhighlightCell);
        this.dt.subscribe("editorSaveEvent", this.onEditorSavedEvent, this);
        this.dt.subscribe("checkboxClickEvent", this.onCheckboxClickEvent, this);
        this.dt.subscribe("radioClickEvent", this.onRadioClickEvent, this);
        this.dt.subscribe("dropdownChangeEvent", this.onDropdownChangeEvent, this);
    }
    this.dt.subscribe("columnSortEvent", this.onColumnSortEvent, this);
    if (controlId != null) {
        var labelControl = new YAHOO.util.Element(controlId);
        switch (this.type) {
        case 'adjustable':
            labelControl.on('click', SelectionDataTableAddRow, this);
            break;
        case 'selectable':
            labelControl.on('click', changeAllSelectedRows, this);
            break;
        }
    }
}
;function SelectionDataTableSetColumnDef(obj) {
    this.columnDef = obj;
}
;function SelectionDataTableSetFieldDef(obj) {
    this.fieldDef = obj;
}
;function SelectionDataTableSetRowTemplate(obj) {
    this.rowTemplate = obj;
}
;function SelectionDataTableAddRow(e, obj) {
    if (typeof obj.getRowTemplateMethod == "function") {
        obj.rowTemplate = obj.getRowTemplateMethod();
    }
    obj.dt.addRow(obj.rowTemplate, 0);
    if (obj.refreshSectionSummary) {
        obj.refreshSectionSummary();
    }
    obj.syncHiddenFields();
}
;function changeAllSelectedRows(e, obj) {
    var records = obj.dt.getRecordSet().getRecords();
    var setValue = null;
    if (document.getElementById(e.srcElement.id).checked) {
        setValue = "true";
    }
    for (i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
        if (records[i].getData("selected") != "disabled" && records[i].getData("selected") != "hidden") {
            obj.dt.getRecordSet().updateKey(records[i], "selected", setValue);
        }
    }
    obj.refreshView();
}
function SelectionDataTableLength() {
    var recordSet = this.dt.getRecordSet();
    return recordSet.getLength();
}
function SelectionDataTableSelected() {
    if (this.type === "adjustable") {
        return this.length();
    } else {
        var selected = 0;
        var records = this.dt.getRecordSet().getRecords();
        for (i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
            if (records[i].getData("selected") == "true") {
                selected++;
            }
        }
        return selected;
    }
}
function SelectionDataTable(container, htmlTable, refreshSectionSummary, properties) {
    this.container = container;
    this.htmlTable = htmlTable;
    this.refreshSectionSummary = refreshSectionSummary;
    this.externalAfterSavedHandler = null;
    this.columnDef = null;
    this.fieldDef = null;
    this.ds = null;
    this.dt = null;
    this.scrolling = false;
    this.height = null;
    this.width = null;
    this.rowTemplate = null;
    this.getRowTemplateMethod = null;
    this.type = "standard";
    this.allowEditing = false;
    this.confirmDelete = false;
    this.sortedBy = null;
    this.skinCode = "default";
    this.errorData = null;
    if (properties != null) {
        if (properties.type != null) {
            this.type = properties.type;
        }
        if (properties.allowEditing != null) {
            this.allowEditing = properties.allowEditing;
        }
        if (properties.confirmDelete != null) {
            this.confirmDelete = properties.confirmDelete;
        }
        if (properties.scrolling != null) {
            this.scrolling = properties.scrolling;
        }
        if (properties.height != null) {
            this.height = properties.height;
        }
        if (properties.width != null) {
            this.width = properties.width;
        }
        if (properties.sortedBy != null) {
            this.sortedBy = properties.sortedBy;
        }
        if (properties.skinCode != null) {
            this.skinCode = properties.skinCode;
        }
    }
    this.render = SelectionDataTableRender;
    this.setFieldDef = SelectionDataTableSetFieldDef;
    this.setColumnDef = SelectionDataTableSetColumnDef;
    this.setRowTemplate = SelectionDataTableSetRowTemplate;
    this.length = SelectionDataTableLength;
    this.selectedLength = SelectionDataTableSelected;
}
;SelectionDataTable.prototype.setGetRowTemplateMethod = function(getRowTemplateMethod) {
    this.getRowTemplateMethod = getRowTemplateMethod;
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.getCellValue = function(rowIndex, key) {
    var theYuiTableObj = this.dt;
    var recordSet = theYuiTableObj.getRecordSet();
    var data = null;
    var record = recordSet.getRecord(rowIndex);
    if (record != null) {
        data = record.getData(key);
    }
    return data;
}
SelectionDataTable.prototype.setCellValue = function(rowIndex, key, value) {
    var theYuiTableObj = this.dt;
    var recordSet = theYuiTableObj.getRecordSet();
    var record = recordSet.getRecord(rowIndex);
    if (record != null) {
        record.setData(key, value);
        this.refreshView();
    } else {
        alert("Unable to set the record data");
    }
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.setCellValueByCount = function(recordCount, key, value) {
    var theYuiTableObj = this.dt;
    var records = theYuiTableObj.getRecordSet().getRecords();
    for (i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
        if (records[i].getCount() == recordCount) {
            records[i].setData(key, value);
            this.refreshView();
            return;
        }
    }
    alert("Unable to set the record data");
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.highlightErrors = function() {
    if (this.errorData != null) {
        var theYuiTableObj = this.dt;
        var records = theYuiTableObj.getRecordSet().getRecords();
        var columnSet = theYuiTableObj.getColumnSet();
        var tableWithoutRecordFound = false;
        var cells = this.errorData.cells;
        for (i = 0; i < cells.length; i++) {
            var recordIndex = cells[i].row;
            var errorRecord = null;
            for (var j = 0; j < records.length; j++) {
                var id = records[j].getData("id");
                if (id.lastIndexOf("." + recordIndex) > -1) {
                    errorRecord = records[j];
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (errorRecord != null) {
                var columnKey = cells[i].column;
                var oColumn = columnSet.getColumn(columnKey);
                var cellEl = theYuiTableObj.getTdEl({
                    record: errorRecord,
                    column: oColumn
                });
                YAHOO.util.Dom.addClass(cellEl, 'error');
            } else {
                tableWithoutRecordFound = true;
            }
        }
        if (tableWithoutRecordFound) {
            YAHOO.util.Dom.addClass(theYuiTableObj.getTableEl(), 'error');
        }
    }
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.highlightRowError = function(rowIndex) {
    var theYuiTableObj = this.dt;
    var rowEl = theYuiTableObj.getTrEl(rowIndex);
    YAHOO.util.Dom.addClass(rowEl, 'error');
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.removeError = function(rowIndex, columnKey) {
    if (this.errorData != null) {
        var cells = this.errorData.cells;
        if (cells != null) {
            var indexOfCellToBeRemoved;
            for (var i = 0; i < cells.length; i++) {
                if (cells[i].row == rowIndex && cells[i].column == columnKey) {
                    indexOfCellToBeRemoved = i;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (indexOfCellToBeRemoved != null && indexOfCellToBeRemoved >= 0) {
                cells.splice(indexOfCellToBeRemoved, 1);
            }
        }
    }
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.setHiddenField = function(id, value) {
    var hiddenEl = document.getElementById(id);
    if (hiddenEl != null) {
        var convertedValue = hlk.util.DateUtil.convertDateToDDMMYYYYWithTime(value, true);
        if (convertedValue == null) {
            convertedValue = "";
        }
        hiddenEl.value = convertedValue;
    }
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.getHiddenFieldValue = function(id) {
    var hiddenEl = document.getElementById(id);
    if (hiddenEl != null) {
        if (hiddenEl.value.strip() == "") {
            return null;
        } else {
            return hiddenEl.value;
        }
    } else {
        return null;
    }
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.onEditorSavedEvent = function(event, obj) {
    var editor = event.editor;
    var newData = event.newData;
    var oldData = event.oldData;
    if (newData != oldData) {
        var record = editor.getRecord();
        var selectedValue = record.getData("selected");
        if (selectedValue != "disabled" && selectedValue != "hidden") {
            record.setData("selected", "true");
        }
        var column = editor.getColumn();
        var sortedBy = obj.dt.get("sortedBy");
        if (sortedBy != null && sortedBy.key == column.key) {
            obj.dt.set("sortedBy", null);
        }
        obj.refreshView();
        var cellEl = obj.dt.getTdEl({
            record: record,
            column: column
        });
        if (YAHOO.util.Dom.hasClass(cellEl, 'error')) {
            YAHOO.util.Dom.removeClass(cellEl, 'error');
        }
    }
    if (obj.externalAfterSavedHandler != null && typeof obj.externalAfterSavedHandler == "function") {
        obj.externalAfterSavedHandler();
    }
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.onCheckboxClickEvent = function(oArgs, obj) {
    var target = oArgs.target;
    var record = this.getRecord(target);
    var column = this.getColumn(target);
    var rowId = record.getData("id");
    var hiddenElId = rowId + "." + column.key;
    if (target.checked) {
        record.setData(column.key, "true");
        obj.setHiddenField(hiddenElId, "true");
    } else {
        record.setData(column.key, null);
        obj.setHiddenField(hiddenElId, "");
    }
    if (column.key == "selected") {
        if (obj.refreshSectionSummary) {
            obj.refreshSectionSummary();
        }
    }
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.onRadioClickEvent = function(oArgs, obj) {
    var target = oArgs.target;
    var record = this.getRecord(target);
    var column = this.getColumn(target);
    var rowId = record.getData("id");
    var targetHiddenElId = rowId + "." + column.key;
    if (target.checked) {
        record.setData(column.key, "true");
        obj.setHiddenField(targetHiddenElId, "true");
    } else {
        record.setData(column.key, null);
        obj.setHiddenField(targetHiddenElId, "");
    }
    if (column.key == "primary") {
        var records = obj.dt.getRecordSet().getRecords();
        var key = "primary";
        for (i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
            var hiddenElId = records[i].getData("id") + "." + key;
            if (targetHiddenElId != hiddenElId) {
                records[i].setData(key, null);
                obj.setHiddenField(hiddenElId, "");
            }
        }
        if (obj.refreshSectionSummary) {
            obj.refreshSectionSummary();
        }
    }
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.onDropdownChangeEvent = function(oArgs, selectionDataTable) {
    var target = oArgs.target;
    var record = this.getRecord(target);
    var column = this.getColumn(target);
    var rowId = record.getData("id");
    var rowIndex = rowId.substr(rowId.lastIndexOf(".") + 1);
    var hiddenElId = rowId + "." + column.key;
    var selectedValue = record.getData("selected");
    if (selectedValue != "disabled" && selectedValue != "hidden") {
        record.setData("selected", "true");
    }
    var dropDownSelectedValue = target.options[target.selectedIndex].value;
    if (dropDownSelectedValue != "") {
        record.setData(column.key, dropDownSelectedValue);
        selectionDataTable.setHiddenField(hiddenElId, dropDownSelectedValue);
        var cellEl = selectionDataTable.dt.getTdEl({
            record: record,
            column: column
        });
        if (YAHOO.util.Dom.hasClass(cellEl, 'error')) {
            YAHOO.util.Dom.removeClass(cellEl, 'error');
        }
        selectionDataTable.removeError(rowIndex, column.key);
    } else {
        record.setData(column.key, null);
        selectionDataTable.setHiddenField(hiddenElId, "");
    }
    selectionDataTable.refreshView();
}
SelectionDataTable.prototype.onCellUpdateEvent = function(oArgs, theYuiTableObj) {
    var cellEl = theYuiTableObj.getTdEl({
        record: oArgs.record,
        column: oArgs.column
    });
    if (YAHOO.util.Dom.hasClass(cellEl, 'error')) {
        YAHOO.util.Dom.removeClass(cellEl, 'error');
    }
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.sortColumn = function(key, sDir) {
    if (sDir == null) {
        this.dt.sortColumn(this.dt.getColumn(key));
    } else {
        this.dt.sortColumn(this.dt.getColumn(key), sDir);
    }
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.onColumnSortEvent = function(oArgs, obj) {
    obj.syncHiddenFields();
    obj.highlightErrors();
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.syncHiddenFields = function() {
    var records = this.dt.getRecordSet().getRecords();
    for (i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
        for (j in this.fieldDef.fields) {
            var rowId = records[i].getData("id");
            var key = this.fieldDef.fields[j].key;
            if (key == null || key == "undefined") {
                key = this.fieldDef.fields[j];
            }
            var value = records[i].getData(key);
            var hiddenElId = rowId + "." + key;
            if (value == null || (value instanceof Date && isNaN(value)) || (key == "selected" && (value == "disabled" || value == "hidden"))) {
                value = "";
            }
            this.setHiddenField(hiddenElId, value);
        }
    }
}
;
SelectionDataTable.prototype.refreshView = function() {
    this.dt.refreshView();
    this.syncHiddenFields();
    if (this.refreshSectionSummary) {
        this.refreshSectionSummary();
    }
    this.highlightErrors();
}
;
function AddressDialog(displayControl, oConfig) {
    this.display = null;
    this.addrLine1 = null;
    this.addrLine2 = null;
    this.suburb = null;
    this.city = null;
    this.postcode = null;
    this.display = document.getElementById(displayControl);
    this.displayDialog = displayControl + "Diag";
    this.setContent = function() {
        var strDisplay = composeAddressDivContent(this.addrLine1.value, this.addrLine2.value, this.suburb.value, this.city.value, this.postcode.value);
        this.display.innerHTML = strDisplay;
    }
    ;
    this.show = function(invokeSiblingMask) {
        this.dialog.body.document.getElementById(this.displayDialog + ".addrLine1").value = this.addrLine1.value;
        this.dialog.body.document.getElementById(this.displayDialog + ".addrLine2").value = this.addrLine2.value;
        this.dialog.body.document.getElementById(this.displayDialog + ".suburb").value = this.suburb.value;
        this.dialog.body.document.getElementById(this.displayDialog + ".city").value = this.city.value;
        this.dialog.body.document.getElementById(this.displayDialog + ".postcode").value = this.postcode.value;
        this.dialog.show();
        if (invokeSiblingMask != false) {
            var parentWindow = window.parent;
            for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
                if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].mask == "function") {
                    parentWindow.frames[i].mask();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    ;
    this.handleSubmit = function(e, obj, invokeSiblingMask) {
        var add1 = obj.dialog.body.document.getElementById(obj.displayDialog + ".addrLine1").value;
        var city = obj.dialog.body.document.getElementById(obj.displayDialog + ".city").value;
        if (add1 == "" || city == "") {
            alert("Please specify a minimum of Address line 1 and city.");
        } else {
            obj.addrLine1.value = add1;
            obj.addrLine2.value = obj.dialog.body.document.getElementById(obj.displayDialog + ".addrLine2").value;
            obj.suburb.value = obj.dialog.body.document.getElementById(obj.displayDialog + ".suburb").value;
            obj.city.value = city;
            obj.postcode.value = obj.dialog.body.document.getElementById(obj.displayDialog + ".postcode").value;
            obj.setContent();
            obj.dialog.destroy();
            if (obj.display) {
                obj.display.focus();
            }
        }
        if (invokeSiblingMask != false) {
            var parentWindow = window.parent;
            for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
                if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].unmask == "function") {
                    parentWindow.frames[i].unmask();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    ;
    this.handleCancel = function(e, obj, invokeSiblingMask) {
        obj.dialog.destroy();
        if (obj.display) {
            obj.display.focus();
        }
        if (invokeSiblingMask != false) {
            var parentWindow = window.parent;
            for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
                if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].unmask == "function") {
                    parentWindow.frames[i].unmask();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    ;
    if (oConfig.addressLine1 != null)
        this.addrLine1 = document.getElementById(oConfig.addressLine1);
    if (oConfig.addressLine2 != null)
        this.addrLine2 = document.getElementById(oConfig.addressLine2);
    if (oConfig.suburb != null)
        this.suburb = document.getElementById(oConfig.suburb);
    if (oConfig.city != null)
        this.city = document.getElementById(oConfig.city);
    if (oConfig.postcode != null)
        this.postcode = document.getElementById(oConfig.postcode);
    this.dialog = new YAHOO.widget.Dialog(this.displayDialog,{
        height: '250px',
        width: '400px',
        fixedcenter: true,
        visible: false,
        modal: true,
        constraintoviewport: true,
        buttons: [{
            text: "Update",
            handler: {
                fn: this.handleSubmit,
                obj: this
            },
            isDefault: true
        }, {
            text: "Cancel",
            handler: {
                fn: this.handleCancel,
                obj: this
            }
        }]
    });
    this.dialog.setHeader('Update address details:');
    this.dialog.setBody('<form>\
						<div class="dialogContainer">\
							<div class="rowDiv">\
								<div class="oneColDiv">Address Line 1:<span class="requiredField">*</span></div>\
								<div class="oneColDiv"><input type="textbox" class="fullWidth" id="' + this.displayDialog + '.addrLine1" /></div>\
							</div>\
							<div class="rowDiv">\
								<div class="oneColDiv">Address Line 2:</div>\
								<div class="oneColDiv"><input type="textbox" class="fullWidth" id="' + this.displayDialog + '.addrLine2" /></div>\
							</div>\
							<div class="rowDiv">\
								<div class="oneColDiv">Suburb:</div>\
								<div class="oneColDiv"><input type="textbox" class="fullWidth" id="' + this.displayDialog + '.suburb" /></div>\
							</div>\
							<div class="rowDiv">\
								<div class="oneColDiv">City:<span class="requiredField">*</span></div>\
								<div class="oneColDiv"><input type="textbox" class="fullWidth" id="' + this.displayDialog + '.city" /></div>\
							</div>\
							<div class="rowDiv">\
								<div class="oneColDiv">Postcode:</div>\
								<div class="oneColDiv"><input type="textbox" class="halfWidth" id="' + this.displayDialog + '.postcode" /></div>\
							</div>\
						</div>\
	</form>');
    this.dialog.setFooter('<span id="buttonSpan" class="button-group"></span>');
    this.dialog.render(document.body);
}
;function displayAddressDivContent(displayControlId, addrLine1Id, addrLine2Id, suburbId, cityId, postcodeId) {
    var addrLine1 = document.getElementById(addrLine1Id).value;
    var addrLine2 = document.getElementById(addrLine2Id).value;
    var suburb = document.getElementById(suburbId).value;
    var city = document.getElementById(cityId).value;
    var postcode = document.getElementById(postcodeId).value;
    var strDisplay = composeAddressDivContent(addrLine1, addrLine2, suburb, city, postcode);
    var displayControl = document.getElementById(displayControlId);
    displayControl.innerHTML = strDisplay;
}
function composeAddressDivContent(addrLine1, addrLine2, suburb, city, postcode) {
    var strDisplay = addrLine1;
    if (addrLine2 != "") {
        if (addrLine1 != "")
            strDisplay = strDisplay + ", ";
        strDisplay = strDisplay + addrLine2;
    }
    if (suburb != "") {
        if (addrLine1 != "" || addrLine2 != "")
            strDisplay = strDisplay + ", ";
        strDisplay = strDisplay + suburb;
    }
    strDisplay = strDisplay + "<br>" + city;
    if (postcode != "") {
        if (city != "")
            strDisplay = strDisplay + ", ";
        strDisplay = strDisplay + postcode;
    }
    return strDisplay;
}
function NameDialog(displayControl, updateSessionSummary, oConfig) {
    this.display = null;
    this.first = null;
    this.second = null;
    this.last = null;
    this.preferred = null;
    this.title = null;
    this.full = null;
    this.display = document.getElementById(displayControl);
    this.updateSessionSummary = updateSessionSummary;
    this.displayDialog = displayControl + "Diag";
    this.setContent = function() {
        var strDisplay = null;
        if (this.preferred != null) {
            strDisplay = composeNameDivContent(this.first.value, this.second.value, this.last.value, this.preferred.value);
        } else {
            strDisplay = composeNameDivContent(this.first.value, this.second.value, this.last.value);
        }
        this.display.innerHTML = strDisplay;
    }
    ;
    this.show = function(invokeSiblingMask) {
        this.dialog.body.document.getElementById(this.displayDialog + ".first").value = this.first.value;
        this.dialog.body.document.getElementById(this.displayDialog + ".second").value = this.second.value;
        this.dialog.body.document.getElementById(this.displayDialog + ".last").value = this.last.value;
        if (this.preferred != null) {
            this.dialog.body.document.getElementById(this.displayDialog + ".preferred").value = this.preferred.value;
        }
        this.dialog.show();
        if (invokeSiblingMask != false) {
            var parentWindow = window.parent;
            for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
                if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].mask == "function") {
                    parentWindow.frames[i].mask();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    ;
    this.handleSubmit = function(e, obj, invokeSiblingMask) {
        var first = obj.dialog.body.document.getElementById(obj.displayDialog + ".first").value;
        var last = obj.dialog.body.document.getElementById(obj.displayDialog + ".last").value;
        if (first == "" || last == "") {
            alert("Please specify a minimum of first and last name.");
        } else {
            obj.first.value = first;
            obj.second.value = obj.dialog.body.document.getElementById(obj.displayDialog + ".second").value;
            obj.last.value = last;
            if (obj.preferred != null) {
                obj.preferred.value = obj.dialog.body.document.getElementById(obj.displayDialog + ".preferred").value;
            }
            obj.dialog.destroy();
            obj.setContent();
            if (obj.updateSessionSummary) {
                obj.updateSessionSummary();
            }
            if (obj.display) {
                obj.display.focus();
            }
        }
        if (invokeSiblingMask != false) {
            var parentWindow = window.parent;
            for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
                if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].unmask == "function") {
                    parentWindow.frames[i].unmask();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    ;
    this.handleCancel = function(e, obj, invokeSiblingMask) {
        obj.dialog.destroy();
        if (obj.display) {
            obj.display.focus();
        }
        if (invokeSiblingMask != false) {
            var parentWindow = window.parent;
            for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
                if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].unmask == "function") {
                    parentWindow.frames[i].unmask();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    ;
    if (oConfig.first != null)
        this.first = document.getElementById(oConfig.first);
    if (oConfig.second != null)
        this.second = document.getElementById(oConfig.second);
    if (oConfig.last != null)
        this.last = document.getElementById(oConfig.last);
    if (oConfig.preferred != null)
        this.preferred = document.getElementById(oConfig.preferred);
    if (oConfig.title != null)
        this.title = document.getElementById(oConfig.title);
    if (oConfig.full != null)
        this.full = document.getElementById(oConfig.full).value;
    this.dialog = new YAHOO.widget.Dialog(this.displayDialog,{
        height: '250px',
        width: '400px',
        fixedcenter: true,
        visible: false,
        modal: true,
        constraintoviewport: true,
        buttons: [{
            text: "Update",
            handler: {
                fn: this.handleSubmit,
                obj: this
            },
            isDefault: true
        }, {
            text: "Cancel",
            handler: {
                fn: this.handleCancel,
                obj: this
            }
        }]
    });
    this.dialog.setHeader('Update name details:');
    this.constructBody = function() {
        var body = '<form>\
			<div class="dialogContainer">';
        if (this.full != null && this.full != "") {
            body += '<div class="rowDiv">\
					<div class="oneColDiv">EMRs full name:</div>\
					<div class="oneColDiv">' + this.full + '\
					<img src="images/information.png" title="Information" height="16" width="16" onClick="InformationLightBox(\'Your system may provide the \'Full name\' to be used when launching this form. The First Name, Middle and Last Names will be derived from the full name if it is supplied by the system.<br/><br/>You may update the First, Middle and Last Names on this form for the current submission.\');"/>\
					</div>\
					</div>';
        }
        body += '<div class="rowDiv">\
			<div class="oneColDiv">First name:<span class="requiredField">*</span></div>\
			<div class="oneColDiv"><input type="textbox" class="fullWidth" id="' + this.displayDialog + '.first" /></div>\
			</div>\
			<div class="rowDiv">\
			<div class="oneColDiv">Middle name(s):</div>\
			<div class="oneColDiv"><input type="textbox" class="fullWidth" id="' + this.displayDialog + '.second" /></div>\
			</div>\
			<div class="rowDiv">\
			<div class="oneColDiv">Last name:<span class="requiredField">*</span></div>\
			<div class="oneColDiv"><input type="textbox" class="fullWidth" id="' + this.displayDialog + '.last" /></div>\
			</div>';
        if (oConfig.preferred != null) {
            body += '<div class="rowDiv">\
					<div class="oneColDiv">Preferred name:</div>\
					<div class="oneColDiv"><input type="textbox" class="fullWidth" id="' + this.displayDialog + '.preferred" /></div>\
					</div>';
        }
        body += '</div>\
				</form>';
        return body;
    }
    this.dialog.setBody(this.constructBody());
    this.dialog.setFooter('<span id="buttonSpan" class="button-group"></span>');
    this.dialog.render(document.body);
}
;function displayNameDivContent(displayControlId, firstId, secondId, lastId, preferredId) {
    var first = document.getElementById(firstId).value;
    var second = document.getElementById(secondId).value;
    var last = document.getElementById(lastId).value;
    var preferred = null;
    if (preferredId != null) {
        preferred = document.getElementById(preferredId).value;
    }
    var strDisplay = composeNameDivContent(first, second, last, preferred);
    var displayControl = document.getElementById(displayControlId);
    displayControl.innerHTML = strDisplay;
}
function composeNameDivContent(first, second, last, preferred) {
    var strDisplay = first;
    if (first != "") {
        if (second != "")
            strDisplay = strDisplay + " ";
        strDisplay = strDisplay + second;
    }
    if (last != "") {
        if (first != "" || second != "")
            strDisplay = strDisplay + " ";
        strDisplay = strDisplay + last;
    }
    if (preferred != null && preferred != "") {
        strDisplay = strDisplay + "<br>Preferred: " + preferred;
    }
    return strDisplay;
}
function InformationLightBox(content, title, invokeSiblingMask) {
    this.handleCancel = function(invokeSiblingMask) {
        this.cancel();
        if (invokeSiblingMask != false) {
            var parentWindow = window.parent;
            for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
                if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].unmask == "function") {
                    parentWindow.frames[i].unmask();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    ;
    this.dialog = new YAHOO.widget.Dialog('InformationLightBox',{
        width: "400px",
        fixedcenter: true,
        visible: false,
        modal: true,
        close: false,
        constraintoviewport: true,
        buttons: [{
            text: "Ok",
            handler: handleCancel
        }]
    });
    this.dialog.showEvent.subscribe(function() {
        jQuery(window).on("keydown.informationLightBox", function(event) {
            if (event.which == 8) {
                event.preventDefault();
            }
        });
    });
    this.dialog.hideEvent.subscribe(function() {
        jQuery(window).off("keydown.informationLightBox");
    });
    if (title != null)
        this.dialog.setHeader("<div class='InformationHeader'>" + title + "</div>");
    else
        this.dialog.setHeader("<div class='InformationHeader'>Information:</div>");
    this.dialog.setBody('<form>\
						<div class="informationContainer">\
						' + content + '</div>\
						</form>');
    this.dialog.setFooter('<span id="buttonSpan" class="button-group"></span>');
    this.dialog.render(document.body);
    this.dialog.show();
    if (invokeSiblingMask != false) {
        var parentWindow = window.parent;
        for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
            if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].mask == "function") {
                parentWindow.frames[i].mask();
            }
        }
    }
}
;YAHOO.namespace("hlk.util");
var gRTEditorContainer;
var gRTEditor;
var gEditing = null;
var gOnCloseRTEditor = null;
var gHlkRichtextEditor;
YAHOO.hlk.util.RichTextEditor = function() {
    this.jspellCheckerReady = false;
    this.jspellFirstLoad = true;
    this.externalOnShowHandler;
    this.externalOnHideHandler;
}
;
YAHOO.hlk.util.RichTextEditor.prototype.show = function(target, title) {
    if (gEditing != null) {
        gRTEditor.saveHTML();
        gEditing.innerHTML = gRTEditor.get('element').value;
    }
    gRTEditor.setEditorHTML(target.innerHTML);
    gRTEditor.saveHTML();
    if (title != null) {
        gRTEditor.toolbar.set("titlebar", "Edit " + title);
    } else {
        gRTEditor.toolbar.set("titlebar", "Edit " + target.id);
    }
    gEditing = target;
    gEditing.scrollIntoView();
    checkSpelling();
    gRTEditorContainer.show();
    var x = YAHOO.util.Dom.getX(target);
    var y = YAHOO.util.Dom.getY(target);
    gRTEditorContainer.moveTo(x, y);
    var xy = YAHOO.util.Dom.getXY(target);
    YAHOO.util.Dom.setXY(gRTEditor.get('element_cont').get('element'), xy);
    if (this.externalOnShowHandler != null) {
        this.externalOnShowHandler();
    }
}
;
YAHOO.hlk.util.RichTextEditor.prototype.hide = function(target, title) {
    if (this.externalOnHideHandler != null) {
        this.externalOnHideHandler();
    }
}
;
function initRichTextEditors(onShowHandler, onHideHandler) {
    gHlkRichtextEditor = new YAHOO.hlk.util.RichTextEditor();
    gHlkRichtextEditor.externalOnShowHandler = onShowHandler;
    gHlkRichtextEditor.externalOnHideHandler = onHideHandler;
    var Dom = YAHOO.util.Dom;
    var Event = YAHOO.util.Event;
    var richTextConfig = {
        height: '150px',
        width: '750px',
        animate: true,
        grouplabels: false,
        dompath: true,
        autoHeight: false,
        focusAtStart: true,
        toolbar: {
            collapse: true,
            titlebar: 'Advanced Text Editing',
            buttonType: 'basic',
            buttons: [{
                group: 'fontstyle',
                label: 'Font Name and Size',
                buttons: [{
                    type: 'select',
                    label: 'Arial',
                    value: 'fontname',
                    disabled: true,
                    menu: [{
                        text: 'Arial',
                        checked: true
                    }, {
                        text: 'Arial Black'
                    }, {
                        text: 'Comic Sans MS'
                    }, {
                        text: 'Courier New'
                    }, {
                        text: 'Lucida Console'
                    }, {
                        text: 'Tahoma'
                    }, {

            }

    }, gRTEditor, true);

    function closeRTEditor() {
        gRTEditorContainer.hide();
        Dom.setXY(this.get('element_cont').get('element'), [-99999, -99999]);
        Dom.removeClass(this.toolbar.get('cont').parentNode, 'yui-toolbar-container-collapsed');
        doneSpellChecking();
        gRTEditor.saveHTML();
        gEditing.innerHTML = gRTEditor.get('element').value;
        var hiddenElem = document.getElementById(gEditing.id + ".hidden");
        hiddenElem.value = gEditing.innerHTML;
        if (gOnCloseRTEditor) {
            gOnCloseRTEditor();
        }
        gHlkRichtextEditor.hide();
        gEditing = null;
    }
    ;function invokeRTEditor(ev) {
        var tar = Event.getTarget(ev);
        while (tar.id != 'referralFormBody') {
            if (Dom.hasClass(tar, 'editable')) {
                invokeRTEditorForTarget(tar);
            }
            tar = tar.parentNode;
        }
    }
