function invokeRTEditorForTargetId(targetId, title) {
    invokeRTEditorForTargetId2(targetId, title, null);
}
function invokeRTEditorForTargetId2(targetId, title, onCloseRTEditor) {
    gOnCloseRTEditor = onCloseRTEditor;
    var tar = document.getElementById(targetId);
    invokeRTEditorForTarget(tar, title);
}
function invokeRTEditorForTarget(target, title) {
    gHlkRichtextEditor.show(target, title);
}
function jspellPostInit() {
    gHlkRichtextEditor.jspellCheckerReady = true;
}
function checkSpelling() {
    jspellRealtime = true;
    jspellAutoAttach = true;
    if (gHlkRichtextEditor.jspellCheckerReady) {
        if (!jspellIsAttached()) {
            jspellAttach();
        } else if (gHlkRichtextEditor.jspellFirstLoad) {
            if (jspellIsAttached()) {
                jspellDetach();
            }
            jspellAttach();
            gHlkRichtextEditor.jspellFirstLoad = false;
        }
    } else {
        setTimeout(checkSpelling, 500);
    }
}
function doneSpellChecking() {
    if (jspellIsAttached()) {
        jspellDetach();
    }
}
function syncHiddenField(elementId) {
    var contentEditableEl = document.getElementById(elementId);
    var hiddenField = document.getElementById(elementId + ".hidden");
    if ($j.trim($j(contentEditableEl).text()) == "") {
        hiddenField.value = "";
    } else {
        var contentInnerHtml = contentEditableEl.innerHTML;
        hiddenField.value = contentInnerHtml.replace(/[\n\r]+/g, '');
    }
    if (typeof $j(hiddenField).valid == "function") {
        $j(hiddenField).valid();
    }
}
var niftyOk = (document.getElementById && document.createElement && Array.prototype.push);
var niftyCss = false;
String.prototype.find = function(what) {
    return (this.indexOf(what) >= 0 ? true : false);
}
var oldonload = window.onload;
if (typeof (NiftyLoad) != 'function')
    NiftyLoad = function() {}
    ;
if (typeof (oldonload) == 'function')
    window.onload = function() {
        oldonload();
        AddCss();
        NiftyLoad()
    }
    ;
else
    window.onload = function() {
        AddCss();
        NiftyLoad()
    }
    ;
function AddCss() {
    niftyCss = true;
    var l = CreateEl("link");
    l.setAttribute("type", "text/css");
    l.setAttribute("rel", "stylesheet");
    l.setAttribute("href", "css/common/niftyCorners.css");
    l.setAttribute("media", "screen");
    document.getElementsByTagName("head")[0].appendChild(l);
}
function Nifty(selector, options) {
    if (niftyOk == false)
        return;
    if (niftyCss == false)
        AddCss();
    var i, v = selector.split(","), h = 0;
    if (options == null)
        options = "";
    if (options.find("fixed-height"))
        h = getElementsBySelector(v[0])[0].offsetHeight;
    for (i = 0; i < v.length; i++)
        Rounded(v[i], options);
    if (options.find("height"))
        SameHeight(selector, h);
}
function Rounded(selector, options) {
    var i, top = "", bottom = "", v = new Array();
    if (options != "") {
        options = options.replace("left", "tl bl");
        options = options.replace("right", "tr br");
        options = options.replace("top", "tr tl");
        options = options.replace("bottom", "br bl");
        options = options.replace("transparent", "alias");
        if (options.find("tl")) {
            top = "both";
            if (!options.find("tr"))
                top = "left";
        } else if (options.find("tr"))
            top = "right";
        if (options.find("bl")) {
            bottom = "both";
            if (!options.find("br"))
                bottom = "left";
        } else if (options.find("br"))
            bottom = "right";
    }
    if (top == "" && bottom == "" && !options.find("none")) {
        top = "both";
        bottom = "both";
    }
    v = getElementsBySelector(selector);
    for (i = 0; i < v.length; i++) {
        FixIE(v[i]);
        if (top != "")
            AddTop(v[i], top, options);
        if (bottom != "")
            AddBottom(v[i], bottom, options);
    }
}
function AddTop(el, side, options) {
    var d = CreateEl("b"), lim = 4, border = "", p, i, btype = "r", bk, color;
    d.style.marginLeft = "-" + getPadding(el, "Left") + "px";
    d.style.marginRight = "-" + getPadding(el, "Right") + "px";
    if (options.find("alias") || (color = getBk(el)) == "transparent") {
        color = "transparent";
        bk = "transparent";
        border = getParentBk(el);
        btype = "t";
    } else {
        bk = getParentBk(el);
        border = Mix(color, bk);
    }
    d.style.background = bk;
    d.className = "niftycorners";
    p = getPadding(el, "Top");
    if (options.find("small")) {
        d.style.marginBottom = (p - 2) + "px";
        btype += "s";
        lim = 2;
    } else if (options.find("big")) {
        d.style.marginBottom = (p - 10) + "px";
        btype += "b";
        lim = 8;
    } else
        d.style.marginBottom = (p - 5) + "px";
    for (i = 1; i <= lim; i++)
        d.appendChild(CreateStrip(i, side, color, border, btype));
    el.style.paddingTop = "0";
    el.insertBefore(d, el.firstChild);
}
function AddBottom(el, side, options) {
    var d = CreateEl("b"), lim = 4, border = "", p, i, btype = "r", bk, color;
    d.style.marginLeft = "-" + getPadding(el, "Left") + "px";
    d.style.marginRight = "-" + getPadding(el, "Right") + "px";
    if (options.find("alias") || (color = getBk(el)) == "transparent") {
        color = "transparent";
        bk = "transparent";
        border = getParentBk(el);
        btype = "t";
    } else {
        bk = getParentBk(el);
        border = Mix(color, bk);
    }
    d.style.background = bk;
    d.className = "niftycorners";
    p = getPadding(el, "Bottom");
    if (options.find("small")) {
        d.style.marginTop = (p - 2) + "px";
        btype += "s";
        lim = 2;
    } else if (options.find("big")) {
        d.style.marginTop = (p - 10) + "px";
        btype += "b";
        lim = 8;
    } else
        d.style.marginTop = (p - 5) + "px";
    for (i = lim; i > 0; i--)
        d.appendChild(CreateStrip(i, side, color, border, btype));
    el.style.paddingBottom = 0;
    el.appendChild(d);
}
function CreateStrip(index, side, color, border, btype) {
    var x = CreateEl("b");
    x.className = btype + index;
    x.style.backgroundColor = color;
    x.style.borderColor = border;
    if (side == "left") {
        x.style.borderRightWidth = "0";
        x.style.marginRight = "0";
    } else if (side == "right") {
        x.style.borderLeftWidth = "0";
        x.style.marginLeft = "0";
    }
    return (x);
}
function CreateEl(x) {
    return (document.createElement(x));
}
function FixIE(el) {
    if (el.currentStyle != null && el.currentStyle.hasLayout != null && el.currentStyle.hasLayout == false)
        el.style.display = "inline-block";
}
function SameHeight(selector, maxh) {
    var i, v = selector.split(","), t, j, els = [], gap;
    for (i = 0; i < v.length; i++) {
        t = getElementsBySelector(v[i]);
        els = els.concat(t);
    }
    for (i = 0; i < els.length; i++) {
        if (els[i].offsetHeight > maxh)
            maxh = els[i].offsetHeight;
        els[i].style.height = "auto";
    }
    for (i = 0; i < els.length; i++) {
        gap = maxh - els[i].offsetHeight;
        if (gap > 0) {
            t = CreateEl("b");
            t.className = "niftyfill";
            t.style.height = gap + "px";
            nc = els[i].lastChild;
            if (nc.className == "niftycorners")
                els[i].insertBefore(t, nc);
            else
                els[i].appendChild(t);
        }
    }
}
function getElementsBySelector(selector) {
    var i, j, selid = "", selclass = "", tag = selector, tag2 = "", v2, k, f, a, s = [], objlist = [], c;
    if (selector.find("#")) {
        if (selector.find(" ")) {
            s = selector.split(" ");
            var fs = s[0].split("#");
            if (fs.length == 1)
                return (objlist);
            f = document.getElementById(fs[1]);
            if (f) {
                v = f.getElementsByTagName(s[1]);
                for (i = 0; i < v.length; i++)
                    objlist.push(v[i]);
            }
            return (objlist);
        } else {
            s = selector.split("#");
            tag = s[0];
            selid = s[1];
            if (selid != "") {
                f = document.getElementById(selid);
                if (f)
                    objlist.push(f);
                return (objlist);
            }
        }
    }
    if (selector.find(".")) {
        s = selector.split(".");
        tag = s[0];
        selclass = s[1];
        if (selclass.find(" ")) {
            s = selclass.split(" ");
            selclass = s[0];
            tag2 = s[1];
        }
    }
    var v = document.getElementsByTagName(tag);
    if (selclass == "") {
        for (i = 0; i < v.length; i++)
            objlist.push(v[i]);
        return (objlist);
    }
    for (i = 0; i < v.length; i++) {
        c = v[i].className.split(" ");
        for (j = 0; j < c.length; j++) {
            if (c[j] == selclass) {
                if (tag2 == "")
                    objlist.push(v[i]);
                else {
                    v2 = v[i].getElementsByTagName(tag2);
                    for (k = 0; k < v2.length; k++)
                        objlist.push(v2[k]);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return (objlist);
}
function getParentBk(x) {
    var el = x.parentNode, c;
    while (el.tagName.toUpperCase() != "HTML" && (c = getBk(el)) == "transparent")
        el = el.parentNode;
    if (c == "transparent")
        c = "#FFFFFF";
    return (c);
}
function getBk(x) {
    var c = getStyleProp(x, "backgroundColor");
    if (c == null || c == "transparent" || c.find("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)"))
        return ("transparent");
    if (c.find("rgb"))
        c = rgb2hex(c);
    return (c);
}
function getPadding(x, side) {
    var p = getStyleProp(x, "padding" + side);
    if (p == null || !p.find("px"))
        return (0);
    return (parseInt(p));
}
function getStyleProp(x, prop) {
    if (x.currentStyle)
        return (x.currentStyle[prop]);
    if (document.defaultView.getComputedStyle)
        return (document.defaultView.getComputedStyle(x, '')[prop]);
    return (null);
}
function rgb2hex(value) {
    var hex = "", v, h, i;
    var regexp = /([0-9]+)[, ]+([0-9]+)[, ]+([0-9]+)/;
    var h = regexp.exec(value);
    for (i = 1; i < 4; i++) {
        v = parseInt(h[i]).toString(16);
        if (v.length == 1)
            hex += "0" + v;
        else
            hex += v;
    }
    return ("#" + hex);
}
function Mix(c1, c2) {
    var i, step1, step2, x, y, r = new Array(3);
    if (c1.length == 4)
        step1 = 1;
    else
        step1 = 2;
    if (c2.length == 4)
        step2 = 1;
    else
        step2 = 2;
    for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        x = parseInt(c1.substr(1 + step1 * i, step1), 16);
        if (step1 == 1)
            x = 16 * x + x;
        y = parseInt(c2.substr(1 + step2 * i, step2), 16);
        if (step2 == 1)
            y = 16 * y + y;
        r[i] = Math.floor((x * 50 + y * 50) / 100);
        r[i] = r[i].toString(16);
        if (r[i].length == 1)
            r[i] = "0" + r[i];
    }
    return ("#" + r[0] + r[1] + r[2]);
}
var hlk = hlk || {};
hlk.eref = hlk.eref || {};
hlk.eref.MessageConstants = {
    PARK_SUCCESSFUL: "Form parked successfully. Please note that attachments selected from your PC need to be re-attached  when resuming the parked form."
};
if (hlk == null || typeof (hlk) != "object") {
    var hlk = new Object();
}
if (hlk.eref == null || typeof (hlk.eref) != "object") {
    hlk.eref = new Object();
}
hlk.eref.InvokeSystemViewer = {
    previewDocument: function(patientDocumentType, referenceId) {
        var patientDocument = null;
        if (hlk.eref.PatientDocument.DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT_TYPE == patientDocumentType) {
            patientDocument = referralForm.getReport(referenceId);
        } else if (hlk.eref.PatientDocument.SCANNED_DOCUMENT_TYPE == patientDocumentType) {
            patientDocument = referralForm.getScannedDocument(referenceId);
        } else {
            alert(patientDocumentType + " is not supported. Preview not available.");
        }
        if (patientDocument == null || patientDocument.content == null) {
            alert("Unable to retrieve the patient document content. Preview is not available");
        } else if (this.isContentCacheAtServer(patientDocument.content)) {
            this.getFileFromCacheServer(patientDocument);
        } else {
            if (patientDocument.cachedFilename != null) {
                this.getFile(patientDocument);
            } else {
                this.cacheFile(patientDocument);
            }
        }
    },
    isContentCacheAtServer: function(text) {
        var pattern = /cacheAtServer{.+}/;
        var result = pattern.test(text);
        return result;
    },
    cacheFile: function(patientDocument) {
        if (patientDocument != null) {
            $("name").value = patientDocument.name;
            $("mimetype").value = patientDocument.mimeType;
            $("content").value = patientDocument.content;
            $("actionType").value = "cacheFile";
            var previewDocumentForm = $("previewPatientDocumentForm");
            var params = Form.serialize(previewDocumentForm);
            new Ajax.Request(previewDocumentForm.action,{
                method: 'post',
                parameters: params,
                onSuccess: this.cacheFileSuccessHandler.bindAsEventListener(this, patientDocument),
                onFailure: this.cacheFileErrorHandler.bind(this)
            });
        } else {
            hlk.eref.Fn.hideActivityPanel();
            alert("Unable to retrieve the patient document content. Preview is not available.");
        }
    },
    cacheFileSuccessHandler: function(response, patientDocument) {
        referralForm.patientDocsContentPreviewCached = true;
        var cachedInfoTxt = null;
        try {
            cachedInfoTxt = "(" + response.responseText + ")";
            var cachedInfo = eval(cachedInfoTxt);
            if (cachedInfo != null && cachedInfo.filename != null) {
                patientDocument.cachedFilename = cachedInfo.filename;
                this.getFile(patientDocument);
            } else {
                hlk.eref.Fn.hideActivityPanel();
                alert("Error occurred while processing document for preview:  cached filename is null. " + response);
            }
        } catch (e) {
            hlk.eref.Fn.hideActivityPanel();
            this.displayErrorMessage("Error occurred while processing document for preview. Response: " + cachedInfoTxt + "<br>Error: " + e.message);
        }
    },
    cacheFileErrorHandler: function(response) {
        this.displayErrorMessage("Error occurred while processing document for preview: " + response.responseText);
    },
    getFileFromCacheServer: function(patientDocument) {
        hlk.eref.Fn.hideActivityPanel();
        if (patientDocument != null) {
            $("previewPatientDocumentForm").action = "services/getCachedFile";
            $("name").value = patientDocument.name;
            $("mimetype").value = patientDocument.mimeType;
            $("content").value = patientDocument.content;
            $("actionType").value = "getFile";
            this.submitForm();
        } else {
            alert("Unable to retrieve the patient document content. Preview is not available.");
        }
    },
    getFile: function(patientDocument) {
        hlk.eref.Fn.hideActivityPanel();
        if (patientDocument != null) {
            $("name").value = patientDocument.cachedFilename;
            $("content").value = "";
            $("actionType").value = "getFile";
            this.submitForm();
        } else {
            alert("Unable to retrieve the patient document content. Preview is not available.");
        }
    },
    streamFile: function(patientDocument) {
        hlk.eref.Fn.hideActivityPanel();
        if (patientDocument != null) {
            $("name").value = patientDocument.name;
            $("mimetype").value = patientDocument.mimeType;
            $("content").value = patientDocument.content;
            $("actionType").value = "streamFile";
            this.submitForm();
        } else {
            alert("Unable to retrieve the patient document content. Preview is not available.");
        }
    },
    deleteCachedFiles: function(deleteCachedFilesSuccessHandler, deleteCachedFilesErrorHandler) {
        $("actionType").value = "deleteCachedFiles";
        var previewDocumentForm = $("previewPatientDocumentForm");
        var params = Form.serialize(previewDocumentForm);
        new Ajax.Request(previewDocumentForm.action,{
            method: 'post',
            parameters: params,
            onSuccess: deleteCachedFilesSuccessHandler,
            onFailure: deleteCachedFilesErrorHandler
        });
    },
    submitForm: function() {
        if (jQuery.browser.mac == true && referralForm.isEmrGenie()) {
            $j("#iframeDialog").dialog(hlk.eref.Fn.getIFrameDialogInitParams("iframeDialogFrame"));
            $j("#iframeDialog").dialog("open");
            $("previewPatientDocumentForm").target = "iframeDialogFrame";
        } else if (jQuery.browser.mac == true && jQuery.browser.webkit == true && jQuery.browser.chrome != true) {
            $("previewPatientDocumentForm").target = "previewPatientDocTarget";
        }
        $("previewPatientDocumentForm").submit();
    },
    displayErrorMessage: function(message) {
        var d = $('errorMessageDiv');
        d.innerHTML = message;
        d.show();
        d.scrollIntoView();
    }
};
if (hlk == null || typeof (hlk) != "object") {
    var hlk = new Object();
}
if (hlk.eref == null || typeof (hlk.eref) != "object") {
    hlk.eref = new Object();
}
hlk.eref.PreviewPatientDocument = {
    previewPanel: null,
    openDocument: function(mimeType, reportType, conceptId, referenceId, escapedReportName) {
        if (this.previewPanel != null) {
            this.previewPanel.hide();
        }
        var reportName = unescape(escapedReportName);
        if (tabExists(hlk.eref.FormConstants.TAB_ATTACHMENTS)) {
            var patientDocument = referralForm.getPatientDocument(reportType, referenceId, conceptId, mimeType, reportName);
            if ("application/rtf" == mimeType || "text/plain" == mimeType) {
                this.processInternalViewableDocument(patientDocument);
            } else {
                this.processOtherDocument(patientDocument);
            }
        }
    },
    processInternalViewableDocument: function(patientDocument) {
        if (patientDocument.previewContent == null) {
            if (patientDocument.content == null) {
                hlk.eref.Fn.showActivityPanel("<span id=\"processing\">Retrieving content</span>", patientDocument.name.escapeHTML());
                referralForm.retrieveSinglePatientDocContent(patientDocument.referenceId, patientDocument.mimeType, patientDocument.type, patientDocument.name, patientDocument.conceptId, this.handleRetrieveSinglePatientDocContentOkResponse.bindAsEventListener(this, patientDocument.referenceId, patientDocument.type, patientDocument.name, patientDocument.mimeType), this.handleRetrieveSinglePatientDocContentErrorResponse.bind(this));
            } else {
                if (patientDocument.mimeType == "text/plain") {
                    var reportNameHtml = patientDocument.name.escapeHTML();
                    this.previewContentDisplay(base64.decode(patientDocument.content), reportNameHtml, patientDocument);
                } else {
                    this.invokePatientDocumentContentConvertionService(patientDocument);
                }
            }
        } else if (patientDocument.previewContent != null) {
            var reportNameHtml = patientDocument.name.escapeHTML();
            this.previewContentDisplay(patientDocument.previewContent, reportNameHtml, patientDocument);
        }
    },
    processOtherDocument: function(patientDocument) {
        if (patientDocument.content == null) {
            hlk.eref.Fn.showActivityPanel("<span id=\"processing\">Retrieving content</span>", patientDocument.name.escapeHTML());
            referralForm.retrieveSinglePatientDocContent(patientDocument.referenceId, patientDocument.mimeType, patientDocument.type, patientDocument.name, patientDocument.conceptId, this.handleRetrievePatientDocContentOkResponseForSystemViewer.bindAsEventListener(this, patientDocument), this.handleRetrieveSinglePatientDocContentErrorResponse.bind(this));
        } else {
            hlk.eref.Fn.showActivityPanel("<span id=\"processing\">Processing content for preview</span>", patientDocument.name.escapeHTML());
            hlk.eref.InvokeSystemViewer.previewDocument(patientDocument.type, patientDocument.referenceId);
        }
    },
    handleRetrievePatientDocContentOkResponseForSystemViewer: function(responseElement, inPatientDocument) {
        var patientDocument = null;
        if (hlk.eref.PatientDocument.DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT_TYPE == inPatientDocument.type) {
            patientDocument = referralForm.getReport(inPatientDocument.referenceId);
        } else {
            patientDocument = referralForm.getScannedDocument(inPatientDocument.referenceId);
        }
        hlk.eref.Fn.showActivityPanel("<span id=\"processing\">Processing content for preview</span>", patientDocument.name.escapeHTML());
        if (patientDocument == null || patientDocument.content == null) {
            hlk.eref.Fn.hideActivityPanel();
            var errorMessage = "Please fix the following errors:<ul>";
            errorMessage += "<li>Unable to retrieve the content for the patient document. Report Type: " + inPatientDocument.type + ", Reference ID: " + inPatientDocument.referenceId + ", Name: " + inPatientDocument.name + ".";
            displayErrorMessage(errorMessage);
        } else {
            hlk.eref.InvokeSystemViewer.previewDocument(patientDocument.type, patientDocument.referenceId);
        }
    },
    invokePatientDocumentContentConvertionService: function(patientDocument) {
        hlk.eref.Fn.showActivityPanel("<span id=\"processing\">Processing content for preview</span>", patientDocument.name.escapeHTML());
        var reportNameHtml = patientDocument.name.escapeHTML();
        var singlePatientDocForm = $("singlePatientDocForm");
        var singlePatientDocContent = document.getElementById("singlePatientDocForm.base64Content");
        singlePatientDocContent.value = patientDocument.content;
        var params = Form.serialize(singlePatientDocForm);
        new Ajax.Request(singlePatientDocForm.action,{
            method: 'post',
            parameters: params,
            onSuccess: this.getPatientDocumentContentConvertionSuccess.bindAsEventListener(this, reportNameHtml, patientDocument),
            onFailure: this.getPatientDocumentContentConvertionFailure
        });
    },
    handleRetrieveSinglePatientDocContentOkResponse: function(responseElement, referenceId, reportType, reportName, mimeType) {
        var errorMessage = "Please fix the following errors:<ul>";
        var patientDocument = null;
        if (hlk.eref.PatientDocument.DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT_TYPE == reportType) {
            patientDocument = referralForm.getReport(referenceId);
        } else {
            patientDocument = referralForm.getScannedDocument(referenceId);
        }
        if (patientDocument.content != null && mimeType == "application/rtf") {
            this.invokePatientDocumentContentConvertionService(patientDocument);
        } else if (patientDocument.content != null && mimeType == "text/plain") {
            var reportNameHtml = patientDocument.name.escapeHTML();
            this.previewContentDisplay(base64.decode(patientDocument.content), reportNameHtml, patientDocument);
        } else {
            hlk.eref.Fn.hideActivityPanel();
            errorMessage += "<li>Unable to retrieve the content for the patient document. Report Type: " + reportType + ", Reference ID: " + referenceId + ", Name: " + reportName + ".";
            displayErrorMessage(errorMessage);
            this.previewPanel = null;
        }
    },
    handleRetrieveSinglePatientDocContentErrorResponse: function(errorText) {
        hlk.eref.Fn.hideActivityPanel();
        displayErrorMessage("Error occurred while retrieving the content of the patient document: " + errorText);
        this.previewPanel = null;
    },
    getPatientDocumentContentConvertionSuccess: function(response, reportNameHtml, patientDocument) {
        patientDocument.previewContent = response.responseText;
        this.previewContentDisplay(patientDocument.previewContent, reportNameHtml, patientDocument);
    },
    getPatientDocumentContentConvertionFailure: function(response) {
        hlk.eref.Fn.hideActivityPanel();
        this.displayPatientDocumentContentConvertionFailure(response.responseText);
    },
    displayPatientDocumentContentConvertionFailure: function(message) {
        hlk.eref.Fn.hideActivityPanel();
        displayErrorMessage("Error occurred while rendering the preview of the patient document: " + message);
        this.previewPanel = null;
    },
    previewContentDisplay: function(htmlContent, reportNameHtml, patientDocument) {
        hlk.eref.Fn.hideActivityPanel();
        var content = htmlContent;
        var title = reportNameHtml;
        if (this.previewPanel == null) {
            this.previewPanel = new YAHOO.widget.Panel('PreviewLightBox',{
                width: "350px",
                visible: false,
                constraintoviewport: true,
                zindex: 4,
                xy: [370, 160],
                underlay: "none"
            });
            this.previewPanel.subscribe("hide", this.handleInternalViewerHideEvent);
        }
        if (title != null)
            this.previewPanel.setHeader("<div class='InformationHeader' style='height:25px;'><div style='float:left;'>" + title + "</div><div class='headerButtonDiv'><input class='button headerButton' type='button' onclick='hlk.eref.PreviewPatientDocument.handleInternalViewerAttachEvent(\"" + patientDocument.referenceId + "\", \"" + patientDocument.type + "\")' value='Attach' /></div></div>");
        else
            this.previewPanel.setHeader("<div class='InformationHeader'>Preview Document</div>");
        this.previewPanel.setBody('<form>\
							<div class="informationContainer">\
							' + content + '</div>\
							</form>');
        this.previewPanel.render(document.body);
        this.previewPanel.show();
        referralForm.maskFormHeaderAndSummary();
    },
    handleInternalViewerHideEvent: function() {
        referralForm.unmaskFormHeaderAndSummary();
    },
    handleInternalViewerAttachEvent: function(referenceId, patientDocumentType) {
        var records = reportsTable.dt.getRecordSet().getRecords();
        for (var i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
            var id = records[i].getData("referenceID");
            var localReportType = records[i].getData("reportType");
            if (id == referenceId && localReportType == patientDocumentType) {
                records[i].setData("selected", "true");
                break;
            }
        }
        reportsTable.refreshView();
        this.previewPanel.hide();
    }
};
if (hlk == null || typeof (hlk) != "object") {
    var hlk = new Object();
}
if (hlk.eref == null || typeof (hlk.eref) != "object") {
    hlk.eref = new Object();
}
hlk.eref.ReferralTypes = {
    serviceToReferralTypeMap: new Hash(),
    defaultServiceReferralTypes: null,
    referralTypeSelected: null,
    createDefaultServiceReferralTypes: function() {
        var defaultServiceReferralTypes = new YAHOO.hlk.eref.ServiceReferralTypes("First Specialist Assessment",null);
        defaultServiceReferralTypes.addReferralType("First Specialist Assessment", "First Specialist Assessment");
        defaultServiceReferralTypes.addReferralType("Follow-up Referral", "Follow-up Referral");
        return defaultServiceReferralTypes;
    },
    setDefaultServiceReferralTypes: function(defaultReferralType, showReferralTypeFor, referralTypeOptionArray) {
        this.defaultServiceReferralTypes = new YAHOO.hlk.eref.ServiceReferralTypes(defaultReferralType,showReferralTypeFor);
        this.defaultServiceReferralTypes.addReferralTypeOptionArray(referralTypeOptionArray);
    },
    addServiceReferralTypes: function(serviceCode, defaultReferralType, showReferralTypeFor, referralTypeOptionArray) {
        var serviceReferralTypes = this.serviceToReferralTypeMap.get(serviceCode);
        if (serviceReferralTypes == null) {
            serviceReferralTypes = new YAHOO.hlk.eref.ServiceReferralTypes(defaultReferralType,showReferralTypeFor);
            this.serviceToReferralTypeMap.set(serviceCode, serviceReferralTypes);
        }
        serviceReferralTypes.addReferralTypeOptionArray(referralTypeOptionArray);
    },
    getServiceReferralTypes: function(serviceCode) {
        var serviceReferralTypes = this.serviceToReferralTypeMap.get(serviceCode);
        if (serviceReferralTypes == null) {
            if (this.defaultServiceReferralTypes == null) {
                this.defaultServiceReferralTypes = this.createDefaultServiceReferralTypes();
            }
            serviceReferralTypes = this.defaultServiceReferralTypes;
        }
        return serviceReferralTypes;
    },
    updateReferralTypeElementByService: function(serviceCode) {
        var serviceReferralTypes = this.defaultServiceReferralTypes;
        if (serviceCode != null) {
            serviceReferralTypes = this.getServiceReferralTypes(serviceCode);
            if (serviceReferralTypes == null) {
                serviceReferralTypes = this.defaultServiceReferralTypes;
            }
        }
        var referralTypeEl = document.getElementById("administrative.referralType");
        if (referralTypeEl != null) {
            this.referralTypeSelected = referralTypeEl.value;
            referralTypeEl.value = "";
            referralTypeEl.options.length = 0;
            if (serviceReferralTypes != null) {
                if (referralTypeEl.size > 1) {
                    if (serviceReferralTypes.referralTypeOptionArray.length < 2) {
                        referralTypeEl.size = 2;
                    } else {
                        referralTypeEl.size = serviceReferralTypes.referralTypeOptionArray.length;
                    }
                }
                for (var i = 0; i < serviceReferralTypes.referralTypeOptionArray.length; i++) {
                    referralTypeEl.options.add(serviceReferralTypes.referralTypeOptionArray[i]);
                    if (this.referralTypeSelected != null && this.referralTypeSelected != "" && serviceReferralTypes.referralTypeOptionArray[i].value == this.referralTypeSelected) {
                        referralTypeEl.value = this.referralTypeSelected;
                    }
                }
                if (serviceReferralTypes.referralTypeOptionArray.length == 1) {
                    referralTypeEl.selectedIndex = 0;
                } else if ("" == referralTypeEl.value && serviceReferralTypes.defaultReferralType != null) {
                    referralTypeEl.value = serviceReferralTypes.defaultReferralType;
                }
            }
        }
    }
};
YAHOO.namespace("hlk.eref");
YAHOO.hlk.eref.ServiceReferralTypes = function(defaultReferralType, showReferralTypeFor) {
    if (showReferralTypeFor != null) {
        this.showReferralTypeFor = showReferralTypeFor;
    } else {
        this.showReferralTypeFor = "Outpatient Appointment";
    }
    this.defaultReferralType = defaultReferralType;
    this.referralTypeOptionArray = new Array();
}
;
YAHOO.hlk.eref.ServiceReferralTypes.prototype.addReferralType = function(referralTypeName, referralTypeValue) {
    var referralTypeOption = document.createElement('option');
    referralTypeOption.text = referralTypeName;
    referralTypeOption.value = referralTypeValue;
    this.referralTypeOptionArray.push(referralTypeOption);
}
;
YAHOO.hlk.eref.ServiceReferralTypes.prototype.addReferralTypeOptionArray = function(referralTypeOptionArray) {
    this.referralTypeOptionArray = referralTypeOptionArray;
}
;
YAHOO.hlk.eref.ServiceReferralTypes.prototype.getReferralTypeOptionArray = function() {
    return this.referralTypeOptionArray;
}
;
YAHOO.hlk.eref.ServiceReferralTypes.prototype.showReferralType = function(referralReason) {
    if (this.showReferralTypeFor != null && this.showReferralTypeFor.indexOf(referralReason) >= 0) {
        return true;
    } else {
        return false;
    }
}

hlk.eref.ReferralReasons = {
    referralReasonsByServiceMap: new Hash(),
    defaultServiceReferralReasons: null,
    referralReasonSelected: null,
    setDefaultServiceReferralReasons: function(defaultReferralReason, referralReasonOptionArray) {
        this.defaultServiceReferralReasons = new YAHOO.hlk.eref.ServiceReferralReasons(defaultReferralReason,referralReasonOptionArray);
    },
    setReferredForToNormalList: function() {
        if (ifExists("administrative.referredFor")) {
            var referredForEl = document.getElementById("administrative.referredFor");
            referredForEl.value = "";
            referredForEl.options.length = 0;
            if (this.defaultServiceReferralReasons != null) {
                var referralReasonOptionArray = this.defaultServiceReferralReasons.referralReasonOptionArray;
                for (var i = 0; i < referralReasonOptionArray.length; i++) {
                    referredForEl.options.add(referralReasonOptionArray[i]);
                    if (this.referralReasonSelected != null && this.referralReasonSelected != "" && referralReasonOptionArray[i].value == this.referralReasonSelected) {
                        referredForEl.value = this.referralReasonSelected;
                    }
                }
                if (referralReasonOptionArray.length == 1) {
                    referredForEl.selectedIndex = 0;
                } else if ("" == referredForEl.value && this.defaultServiceReferralReasons.defaultReferralReason != null) {
                    referredForEl.value = this.defaultServiceReferralReasons.defaultReferralReason;
                }
            }
        }
    },
    setReferralReasonByServiceList: function(serviceCode) {
        var serviceReferralReasons = this.referralReasonsByServiceMap.get(serviceCode);
        if (serviceReferralReasons != null) {
            var referralReasonOptionArray = serviceReferralReasons.referralReasonOptionArray;
            var referredForEl = document.getElementById("administrative.referredFor");
            referredForEl.value = "";
            referredForEl.options.length = 0;
            for (var i = 0; i < referralReasonOptionArray.length; i++) {
                referredForEl.options.add(referralReasonOptionArray[i]);
                if (this.referralReasonSelected != null && this.referralReasonSelected != "" && referralReasonOptionArray[i].value == this.referralReasonSelected) {
                    referredForEl.value = this.referralReasonSelected;
                }
            }
            if (referralReasonOptionArray.length == 1) {
                referredForEl.selectedIndex = 0;
            } else if ("" == referredForEl.value && serviceReferralReasons.defaultReferralReason != null) {
                referredForEl.value = serviceReferralReasons.defaultReferralReason;
            }
        } else {
            this.setReferredForToNormalList();
        }
    },
    serviceCodeHasConfiguredReferralReasonList: function(serviceCode) {
        var map = this.referralReasonsByServiceMap;
        var serviceReferralReasons = map.get(serviceCode);
        if (serviceReferralReasons !== 'undefined' && serviceReferralReasons != null) {
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }
};

    this.referralReasonOptionArray = referralReasonOptionArray;
}

YAHOO.namespace("hlk.eref");
var gAutoParkObj;
YAHOO.hlk.eref.AutoPark = function() {
    if (isEmpty(document.getElementById('autoParkValueInMilliSecs').value) || document.getElementById('autoParkValueInMilliSecs').value == null) {
        this.idleTimeout = 30000;
        this.checkInterval = 10000;
        this.lastIdleTime = new Date();
    } else {
        this.idleTimeout = document.getElementById('autoParkValueInMilliSecs').value;
        this.checkInterval = 10000;
        this.lastIdleTime = new Date();
    }
    this.serializedForm = null;
    this.resetIdle = function() {
        this.contentDocIdle = true;
        this.summaryDocIdle = true;
        this.headerDocIdle = true;
        this.rtEditorIdle = true;
    }
    ;
    this.isIdle = function() {
        if (this.contentDocIdle && this.summaryDocIdle && this.headerDocIdle && this.rtEditorIdle) {
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }
    ;
    this.idleTimeExceeded = function() {
        var currentTime = new Date();
        if (currentTime.getTime() - this.lastIdleTime.getTime() >= this.idleTimeout) {
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }
    ;
    this.formChanged = function() {
        var form = document.getElementById('referralForm');
        var currentSerializedForm = Form.serialize(form);
        if (currentSerializedForm == this.serializedForm) {
            return false;
        } else {
            this.serializedForm = currentSerializedForm;
            return true;
        }
    }
    ;
    this.bindIdleEvents = function() {
        var contentDocActive = function(e, autoParkObj) {
            autoParkObj.contentDocIdle = false;
        };
        var summaryDocActive = function(e, autoParkObj) {
            autoParkObj.summaryDocIdle = false;
        };
        var headerDocActive = function(e, autoParkObj) {
            autoParkObj.headerDocIdle = false;
        };
        var contentDoc = hlk.eref.Fn.getReferralFormContentFrameDocument();
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(contentDoc, "mousemove", contentDocActive, this);
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(contentDoc, "keydown", contentDocActive, this);
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(contentDoc, "mousedown", contentDocActive, this);
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(contentDoc, "mousewheel", contentDocActive, this);
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(contentDoc, "DOMMouseScroll", contentDocActive, this);
        var summaryDoc = hlk.eref.Fn.getReferralFormSummaryFrameDocument();
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(summaryDoc, "mousemove", summaryDocActive, this);
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(summaryDoc, "keydown", summaryDocActive, this);
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(summaryDoc, "mousedown", summaryDocActive, this);
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(summaryDoc, "mousewheel", summaryDocActive, this);
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(summaryDoc, "DOMMouseScroll", summaryDocActive, this);
        var headerDoc = hlk.eref.Fn.getReferralFormHeaderFrameDocument();
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(headerDoc, "mousemove", headerDocActive, this);
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(headerDoc, "keydown", headerDocActive, this);
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(headerDoc, "mousedown", headerDocActive, this);
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(headerDoc, "mousewheel", headerDocActive, this);
        YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(headerDoc, "DOMMouseScroll", headerDocActive, this);
    }
    ;
    this.bindRtEditorIdleEvents = function(rtEditor) {
        var rtEditorActive = function(e, autoParkObj) {
            autoParkObj.rtEditorIdle = false;
        };
        rtEditor.on("editorKeyDown", rtEditorActive, this);
        rtEditor.on("editorMouseDown", rtEditorActive, this);
    }
    ;
    this.resetIdle();
    this.bindIdleEvents();
    this.run = function() {
        var obj = gAutoParkObj;
        clearTimeout(obj.timeoutId);
        if (obj.isIdle() && obj.idleTimeExceeded() && obj.formChanged()) {
            hlk.eref.Fn.getReferralFormContentFrame().parkForm(true);
            this.lastIdleTime = new Date();
        }
        obj.resetIdle();
        obj.timeoutId = setTimeout(obj.run, obj.checkInterval);
    }
    ;
    this.timeoutId = setTimeout(this.run, this.idleTimeout);
}
;

YAHOO.util.Event.throwErrors = true;
var tabManager;
var divMenu;

var gMaxSummaryTextSize = 68;
var gMaxPatientTextSize = 30;

function getTabToInitFunctionMap() {

    var tabToInitFunctionMap = new Hash();

    if (tabExists(hlk.eref.FormConstants.TAB_CLINICAL)) {
        tabToInitFunctionMap.set(hlk.eref.FormConstants.TAB_CLINICAL, initClinicalTab);
    }

    return tabToInitFunctionMap;
}

function pageLoad(isFetchPreview, isShowAttWarningFromParkedForm) {
    // init the platform specific conditions
    if (typeof initPlatform == "function") {
        initPlatform();
    }

    // removed the cached event listeners to avoid exception from executing code from freed script
    referralForm.removeAllEventListeners();

    // Register a default onException listener to always pop-up a message dialog when
    // error occurs.
    Ajax.Responders.register({
        onException: function(requester, e) {
            if (e != null && e != 'undefined') {
                alert(e.message);
            }
        }
    });

    referralForm.isOnPageLoad = true;

    // registers the interceptors from dynamic form before any other processing starts
    if (getFormView() != null) {
        try {
            referralForm.registerDynamicInterceptors("referralForm", formView);
        } catch (err) {
            hlk.util.Dialog.alert(err.message, "Error");
            return;
        }
    }

    if (isFetchPreview) {
        fetchPreview();
    }

    // change summary frame skin in case it's from reload
    hlk.eref.Fn.getHeaderFrame().changeSkin(referralForm.skinCode);
    // change header frame skin in case it's from reload
    hlk.eref.Fn.getSummaryFrame().changeSkin(referralForm.skinCode);

    // this is to force update of the logo after the new URL is retrieved from the service property
    if (referralForm.isFromFacilityReload) {
        referralForm.refreshLogo();
    }

    hlk.util.Dialog.init("yui");

    initMenu();

    // NZF-129: We need to init the date picker first before the other tabs are initialised so that the the date picker can be customised if necessary
    initDatepickerInput();

    var interceptorParam = {};
    referralForm.invokeInterceptors(hlk.form.LifeCycle.PHASE.BEFORE_INIT_TABS, interceptorParam, processOnceBeforeInitTabsInterceptorProcessCompleted);

    function processOnceBeforeInitTabsInterceptorProcessCompleted() {
        // PS-727: moved here so that it can be initialised by the dynamicUtils from the interceptor
        referralForm.sectionTabContainers = initSectionTabContainers();

        // the initTabs() should be called before initTabManager() so that the sectionTabContainers is updated
        initTabs();

        try {
            var interceptorParam = {};
            referralForm.invokeInterceptors(hlk.form.LifeCycle.PHASE.AFTER_INIT_TABS, interceptorParam);
        } catch (err) {
            hlk.util.Dialog.alert(err.message, "Error");
        }

        var currentTabId = initTabManager();

        if (isShowAttWarningFromParkedForm) {
            showAttachmentWarningFromParkedForm();
        }

        if (referralForm.isPrintAndFax()) {
            var message = "This provider's system is not capable of receiving electronic referrals.<br/>";
            message += "After submission you need to print this referral and send by fax or mail.<br/>";
            message += "Please note: if you include attachments you will need to print these separately.";
            showInfoMessage(message);
        }

        // render the current tab first
        var tabToInitFunctionMap = getTabToInitFunctionMap();
        var initFunction = tabToInitFunctionMap.get(currentTabId);
        if (typeof initFunction == "function") {
            tabToInitFunctionMap.unset(currentTabId);
            initFunction();
        }

        // we want to delay the processing of the other tab until the active tab is rendered
        setTimeout(function() {
            delayInit(tabToInitFunctionMap);
        }, 1);
    }
}

function initMenu() {
    divMenu = new FSMenu('divMenu',false,'visibility','visible','hidden');
    divMenu.animations[divMenu.animations.length] = FSMenu.animFade;
    divMenu.animations[divMenu.animations.length] = FSMenu.animSwipeDown;
    divMenu.cssLitClass = 'highlighted';
    divMenu.animInSpeed = 0.2;
    divMenu.animOutSpeed = 1;
}

function initTooltip() {
    $j(function() {
        $j("*[title]").tooltip({
            show: {
                delay: 1000,
                duration: 500
            },
            open: function(event, ui) {
                // AAF-108: Workaround to fix dynamic content tooltip: see comment 28 in http://bugs.jqueryui.com/ticket/8740
                $j(ui.tooltip).siblings(".ui-tooltip").remove();
            }
        });

        $j("iframe[title]").tooltip("disable");
    });
}

function initTabs() {
    for (var i = 0; i < referralForm.tabs.keys().length; i++) {
        var tabName = referralForm.tabs.keys()[i];
        var tabInitFunctionName = tabName + "TabInit";
        var tabInitFunction = window[tabInitFunctionName];
        if (typeof tabInitFunction == "function") {
            tabInitFunction();
        }
    }
}

function initKeyListeners() {
    $("contentColumn").focus();
    hlk.eref.KeyboardAccess.init(tabManager);
}

function initValidationListeners() {
    // Init listener for clearing validation error when value is changed
    function removeErrorIndicatorForFields(objEvent) {
        var jEl = $j(this);
        // due to Form 1.5 view property invokeViewEventsManuallylazy=true, the change event maybe invoked during pageload
        // we want to filter out that since this method should only be run when the user manually trigger the change
        if (referralForm.isOnPageLoadFn()) {
            return;
        }
        var name = jEl.prop("name");
        if (name != null) {
            var namePrefix = name.match(/.*]./);
            if (namePrefix != null) {
                // Clear error for the fields in same group, i.e. group requiredIf
                var jGroupEls = $j("[name^='" + namePrefix + "'].error");
                jGroupEls.removeClass("error");
                jGroupEls.off("change", removeErrorIndicatorForFields);
            } else {
                // Clear individual field and fields for radio button
                var jEls = $j("[name='" + name + "'].error");
                jEls.removeClass("error");
                jEls.off("change", removeErrorIndicatorForFields);
            }
        }
    }

    var jErrElement = $j(":input.error").not("input[type=hidden]");
    jErrElement.on("change", removeErrorIndicatorForFields);

    // For clearing the contenteditable div
    var contentEditableDiv = $j("div.error");
    contentEditableDiv.prop("oldValue", function(i, oldValue) {
        return $j(this).text();
    });
    contentEditableDiv.blur(function(objEvent) {
        if ($j(this).prop("oldValue") != $j(this).text()) {
            $j(this).prop("oldValue", $j(this).text());
            $j(this).removeClass("error");
        }
    });

    // for SmartSelect multi select element
    $j(".SmartSelectElement .error").each(function(index) {
        var jSmartSelectErrorSiblingEl = $j(this);
        var jSmartSelect = hlk.dynamic.smartSelect.getSmartSelectControl(this.name);
        if (jSmartSelect != null && jSmartSelect.prop("multiple") == true) {
            jSmartSelect.on("change.error", function(e) {
                jSmartSelectErrorSiblingEl.removeClass("error");
                $j(this).off("change.error");
            });
        }
    });
}

function initClinicalTab() {
    // invoke the initialisation function for the clinical common section
    if (typeof referralClinicalCommonInit == "function") {
        referralClinicalCommonInit();
    }

    // invoke the initialisation function for the clinical common measurements section
    if (typeof referralClinicalMeasurementsCommonInit == "function") {
        referralClinicalMeasurementsCommonInit();
    }
    //invoke the task reminder init function which is defined in referralClinicalMeasurementsCommon.jsp
    if (typeof referralClinicalTaskReminderInit == "function") {
        referralClinicalTaskReminderInit();
    }

    //call service specific information page load function
    if (typeof ssiPageLoad == "function") {
        ssiPageLoad();
    }
}

function initPlaceHolder() {
    $j("input, textarea").placeholder();
}

/**
 * This function truncates the data in the text fields.
 * TBC as there are still some issues/UX to be defined.
 * @returns
 */
function initMaxLengthValidation() {
    $j("input:text[maxLength]").on("paste", function(e) {
        var value = $j(this).val();
        var clipboardData = hlk.eref.FormUtil.getClipboardData(e);
        if (clipboardData != null && value.length + clipboardData.length > this.maxLength) {
            var feedbackId = this.id + "Feedback";
            var feedbackEl = document.getElementById(feedbackId);
            if (feedbackEl == null) {
                var msg = "Maximum " + this.maxLength + " character(s) only";
                $j("<span id='" + feedbackId + "' class='maxlength-feedback maxlength-full'>" + msg + "</span>").insertAfter(this);
            } else {
                $j(feedbackEl).show();
            }
            $j(this).addClass("maxlength-feedback maxlength-full");

            var pastedData = clipboardData.substring(0, this.maxLength);
            $j(this).prop("oldValue", pastedData);
            $j(this).on("change", function(e) {
                if ($j(this).prop("oldValue") != $j(this).val()) {
                    $j(this).prop("oldValue", $j(this).val());
                    $j(this).removeClass("maxlength-feedback maxlength-full");
                    var feedbackId = this.id + "Feedback";
                    var feedbackEl = document.getElementById(feedbackId);
                    $j(feedbackEl).hide();
                }
            });
        }
    });

    $j("#clinicalContainer textarea[maxlength]").each(function(i, el) {
        var maxLength = jQuery(el).attr("maxLength");
        $j(el).maxlength({
            max: maxLength,
            feedbackText: "",
            onFull: function(overflowing) {
                var msg = "Maximum " + jQuery(this).attr("maxLength") + " character(s) only";
                jQuery(this).next("span").html(msg);
            }
        });
    })
}
;
/**
 * This method is to be called last during the initialisation of the page.
 * @return
 */
function initLast() {
    // EREF-2771 Make image focusable and can be triggered by the keyboard
    $j("img").attr("tabindex", "0").each(function() {
        // Only add an event handler for those that do not have one
        if ($j(this).attr("onkeypress") == null) {
            $j(this).keypress(function(e) {
                if (e.keyCode === 13 || e.keyCode === 32) {
                    // AAF-128: only allow click with "enter" or "space"
                    this.click();
                }
            });
        }
    });

    // Copy the info from SSI to the attachment section
    syncRelevantAttachmentInfo();

    // showFieldIdErrors();
    showYuiTableErrors();

    gAutoParkObj = new YAHOO.hlk.eref.AutoPark();

    $j("textarea.autosize").autosize();
    $j("textarea.singleLineAutosize").autosize();

    initKeyListeners();
    initValidationListeners();
    initTooltip();

    // init placeholder
    initPlaceHolder();

    // init the scroll buttons on the summary frame
    // Need to put this last so that we get the correct doc height 
    if (referralForm.errorSections.length > 0) {
        hlk.eref.Fn.getSummaryFrame().showErrorNavigation();
    } else {
        hlk.eref.Fn.getSummaryFrame().showNormalNavigation();
    }
    hlk.eref.Fn.getSummaryFrame().updateButtons();

    $j(document).ajaxStart(function() {
        displayProcessingMessage();
    });

    $j(document).ajaxStop(function() {
        hideProcessingMessage();
    });

    referralForm.isSubmitting = false;

    // init the consultation notes browser if available
    if (hlk.eref.ConsultationNotesBrowser != null) {
        hlk.eref.ConsultationNotesBrowser.init();
    }

    //hopefully it is ok to set this back to false after page load 
    //looked around and didn't see any code that looked like it would be effected
    //ji Nov 12, 2012
    referralForm.isFromPreview = false;
    referralForm.isFromFacilityReload = false;

    referralForm.triggerEvent(hlk.eref.Event.referralFormContentInitLastDone);
    referralForm.isOnPageLoad = false;
    referralForm.isContentFrameInitCompleted = true;
}

/**
 * This method invokes the init methods passed in from the map.
 * @param tabToInitFunctionMap
 * @return
 */
function delayInit(tabToInitFunctionMap) {
    var initFunctions = tabToInitFunctionMap.values();
    for (var i = 0; i < initFunctions.length; i++) {
        initFunctions[i]();
    }
    initLast();
}

function initDatepickerInput() {
    var datepickerProp = {
        buttonText: "Select date",
        showOn: "both",
        buttonImage: "images/calendar.png",
        buttonImageOnly: true,
        showButtonPanel: true,
        showOtherMonths: true,
        dateFormat: 'dd/mm/yy',
        changeMonth: true,
        changeYear: true,
        yearRange: "1900:+10"
    };

    hlk.util.DateUtil.initDatepicker("input:text.datepicker", datepickerProp);

    var $jDatepickerOnDialog = $j("input:text.datepickerOnDialog");
    datepickerProp.showOn = "button";
    $jDatepickerOnDialog.datepicker(datepickerProp);
}

function getTabManager() {
    return tabManager;
}

function initTabManager() {
    var referralForm = hlk.eref.Fn.getReferralForm();

    var summaryDoc = hlk.eref.Fn.getReferralFormSummaryFrameDocument();

    var tabSummaryColumn = summaryDoc.getElementById("tabsColumn");
    tabManager = new TabManager(summaryDoc,tabSummaryColumn);

    var currentTabContainerId = tabManager.currentTab.container.id;
    // Shows error indicator for the tab
    for (var i = 0; i < referralForm.errorSections.length; i++) {
        var tabContainerId = referralForm.sectionTabContainers.get(referralForm.errorSections[i]);
        tabManager.showErrorIndicator(tabContainerId);
    }
    if (referralForm.isFromPreview || referralForm.isFromSubmit) {
        if (tabManager.getFirstErrorTab() != null) {
            currentTabContainerId = tabManager.getFirstErrorTab().container.id;
        }
    }
    tabManager.setCurrent(currentTabContainerId);

    var tab = tabManager.getTab("clinicalTabContainer");
    if (tab != null) {
        if (tab.onActiveCallback == null) {
            tab.onActiveCallback = function() {
                referralForm.triggerEvent(hlk.eref.Event.clinicalTabOnActive);
            }
        } else {
            var existingActiveCallback = tab.onActiveCallback;
            tab.onActiveCallback = function() {
                if (typeof existingActiveCallback == "function") {
                    existingActiveCallback();
                } else {
                    eval(existingActiveCallback);
                }
                referralForm.triggerEvent(hlk.eref.Event.clinicalTabOnActive);
            }
        }
    }

    // trigger the custom event
    referralForm.triggerEvent(hlk.eref.Event.tabManagerCreated);

    return currentTabContainerId;
}

/**
 * This method is called when the submit button is clicked
 */
function onSubmitTabs() {

    if (typeof referralClinicalCommonOnSubmit == "function") {
        referralClinicalCommonOnSubmit();
    }

    if (typeof referralClinicalMeasurementsCommonOnSubmit == "function") {
        referralClinicalMeasurementsCommonOnSubmit();
    }

    for (var i = 0; i < referralForm.tabs.keys().length; i++) {
        var tabName = referralForm.tabs.keys()[i];
        var tabOnSubmitFunctionName = tabName + "TabOnSubmit";
        var tabOnSubmitFunction = window[tabOnSubmitFunctionName];
        if (typeof tabOnSubmitFunction == "function") {
            tabOnSubmitFunction();
        }
    }
}

/**
 * This method is called when the preview button is clicked.
 */
function onPreviewTabs() {
    if (typeof referralClinicalCommonOnPreview == "function") {
        referralClinicalCommonOnPreview();
    }

    if (typeof referralClinicalMeasurementsCommonOnPreview == "function") {
        referralClinicalMeasurementsCommonOnPreview();
    }

    for (var i = 0; i < referralForm.tabs.keys().length; i++) {
        var tabName = referralForm.tabs.keys()[i];
        var tabOnPreviewFunctionName = tabName + "TabOnPreview";
        var tabOnPreviewFunction = window[tabOnPreviewFunctionName];
        if (typeof tabOnPreviewFunction == "function") {
            tabOnPreviewFunction();
        }
    }
}

function showSuspendSubmissionMessageFromSubmittingForm(hospitalRefs) {
    var message = 'The documents highlighted in red(' + hospitalRefs + ') cannot be attached to the referral, as they have the incorrect File Type (please contact your system vendor to seek a resolution for this issue). Either click: ' + '<p>1. "Continue Submission" and send these documents separately - they will be listed in the "Relevant Files that are not attached" section of the referral, or </p>' + '<p>2. "Cancel" and un-check these documents in the Attachments/Reports Tab</p>';
    hlk.util.DialogYesNo.showYesNoDialog(message, "Warning", onContinueSubmissionHandler, onCancelSubmissionHandler, true, "Continue Submission", "Cancel");

}

function onContinueSubmissionHandler() {
    var referralFormEl = $("referralForm");
    var continueSubmitEl = document.createElement("input");
    continueSubmitEl.setAttribute("name", "fromContinueSubmission");
    continueSubmitEl.setAttribute("type", "hidden");
    continueSubmitEl.setAttribute("value", "true");
    continueSubmitEl.style.display = "none";
    referralFormEl.appendChild(continueSubmitEl);
    hlk.eref.Fn.getHeaderFrame().submit();
}

function onCancelSubmissionHandler() {
    tabManager.showWarningIndicator(hlk.eref.FormConstants.TAB_ATTACHMENTS);
}

function showInfoMessage(message) {
    var infoMsgDiv = document.getElementById("infoMessageDiv");
    if (infoMsgDiv != null) {
        infoMsgDiv.innerHTML = message;
        showHide("infoMessageDiv", "block");
    }
}

function showAttachmentWarningFromParkedForm() {
    var attWarningDiv = document.getElementById("warningMessageDiv");
    if (attWarningDiv != null) {
        attWarningDiv.innerHTML = "Attachments from previous session have to be re-attached if they were selected from your PC. Please refer to the corresponding section for details.";
        showHide("warningMessageDiv", "block");
        tabManager.showWarningIndicator("attachmentsTabContainer");
    }
}

function syncRelevantAttachmentInfo() {
    var relevantAttInfoEl = document.getElementById("clinical.relevantAttachmentInfo");
    if (relevantAttInfoEl != null) {
        var relevantAttInfoEl2 = document.getElementById("attachment.relevantAttachmentInfo");
        relevantAttInfoEl2.innerHTML = relevantAttInfoEl.innerHTML;
    }
}

/* Update section summary text. 
* sectionSummaryDivId is the div id which contains the summary text.
*/
function updateSectionSummary(summaryText, maxTextSize, sectionSummaryDivId) {
    var sectionSummaryDiv = hlk.eref.Fn.getSectionSummaryDiv(sectionSummaryDivId);
    var showSummaryText = summaryText;
    if (summaryText.length > maxTextSize) {
        showSummaryText = summaryText.substring(0, (maxTextSize - 4)) + " ...";
        sectionSummaryDiv.title = summaryText;
    }
    sectionSummaryDiv.innerText = showSummaryText;
}

//get XMLHttpRequest object
function getHttRequest() {
    var httpRequest = null;
    if (window.XMLHttpRequest) {
        // mozilla
        httpRequest = new XMLHttpRequest();
    } else if (window.ActiveXObject) {
        // ie
        try {
            httpRequest = new ActiveXObject("Microsoft.XMLHTTP");
        } catch (e) {
            httpRequest = new ActiveXObject('Msxml2.XMLHTTP');
        }
    }
    if (null == httpRequest) {
        alert("Failed to get XMLHttpRequest object.");
    }
    return httpRequest;
}

function showFieldIdErrors() {
    for (var i = 0; i < referralForm.errorFieldIds.length; i++) {
        var errFieldId = referralForm.errorFieldIds[i];
        var elem = document.getElementById(errFieldId);
        elem.className += elem.className ? ' error' : 'error';
    }
}

function showYuiTableErrors() {
    if (gYuiTableErrors == null || !(gYuiTableErrors instanceof Array)) {
        return;
    }
    var myErrorArray = gYuiTableErrors;
    for (var k = 0; k < myErrorArray.length; k++) {
        var tableError = myErrorArray[k];
        var theTable = gYuiTables.get(tableError.tableName);
        if (theTable == null)
            continue;

        if (theTable.errorData == null) {
            theTable.errorData = tableError;
        } else {
            for (var i = 0; i < tableError.cells.length; i++) {
                var newCellError = tableError.cells[i];
                theTable.errorData.cells[theTable.errorData.cells.length] = newCellError;
            }
        }
        theTable.highlightErrors();
    }
}

function tabExists(sectionId) {
    if (typeof (hlk.eref.Fn.getSectionSummaryDiv(sectionId)) !== 'undefined' && (hlk.eref.Fn.getSectionSummaryDiv(sectionId)) != null) {
        return true;
    } else {
        return false;
    }
}

    return hlk.util.Dialog;
}

 * Gets the dynamic form view json definition instance.
