        return formView;
    } else {
        return null;
    }
}

function sendFormLoadEventLog(referralForm, formModel) {
    function getFieldValue(fieldId, useNativeMethod) {
        var element = null;
        if (useNativeMethod && typeof document.getElementByIdNative === "function") {
            element = document.getElementByIdNative(fieldId);
        } else {
            element = document.getElementById(fieldId);
        }
        return (element != null) ? element.value : "";
    }

    var fCode = "";
    try {
        var facilityEl = document.getElementById("administrative.facility");
        fCode = (facilityEl != null) ? facilityEl.value : "";
    } catch (err) {
        fCode = "";
    }

    var eventLogFields = [["patientDob", true, "patientDob"], ["patientGender", true, "patientGender"], ["patient.indigenousStatus", false, "patientIndigenousStatus"], ["patientResidentialAddressState", true, "patientResidentialAddressState"], ["patientResidentialAddressPostCode", true, "patientResidentialAddressPostCode"], ["serviceViewName", true, "serviceViewName"], ["referrerId", false, "medicareProviderNo"], ["referrer.id.hpii", false, "referrerIdHpii"], ["referrer.id.hpio", false, "referrerIdHpio"], ["referrerFirstName", true, "referrerFirstName"], ["referrerLastName", true, "referrerLastName"], ["referrerOrgName", true, "referrerOrgName"], ["practiceAddressLine1", true, "practiceAddressLine1"], ["practiceAddressLine2", true, "practiceAddressLine2"], ["practiceAddressSuburb", true, "practiceAddressSuburb"], ["practiceAddressState", true, "practiceAddressState"], ["practiceAddressPostCode", true, "practiceAddressPostCode"], ["referrerContactTelephone", true, "referrerContactTelephone"], ["referrerContactFax", true, "referrerContactFax"], ["referrerEmail", true, "referrerEmail"]];

    var eventLogData = {
        "service": referralForm.serviceCode,
        "facilityCode": fCode,
        "referralId": referralForm.formScopeId,
        "emrInterfaceName": referralForm.emrInterfaceName,
        "emrInterfaceVersion": referralForm.emrInterfaceVersion,
        "formMethod": referralForm.formMethod,
        "emrApplicationName": getFieldValue("applicationName", false) || formModel["hiso.pmsApplication"] || "",
        "emrApplicationVersion": getFieldValue("applicationVersion", false) || formModel["hiso.pmsApplicationVersion"] || "",
        "portalMode": referralForm.isPortalMode(),
        "operationMode": getFieldValue("operationMode", false)
    };

    for (var i = 0; i < eventLogFields.length; i++) {
        var fieldConfig = eventLogFields[i];
        eventLogData[fieldConfig[2]] = getFieldValue(fieldConfig[0], fieldConfig[1]);
    }

    hlk.eref.FormUtil.doSendEventLog("formLoad", eventLogData);
}

/*  javascript API for referralForm.jsp to submit or park the form
* 
*  Dependency:  requires prototype.js, domutil.js, stringUtil.js,soapUtil.js
*
*  (c) 2008-2009 Ignatius Feng
*  HealthLink Limited
*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/

function showFieldHelp(baseUrl, fieldCode) {
    var facilityCode = "";
    facilityCode = document.getElementById("facility").value;
    if (facilityCode != null && facilityCode != "") {
        var url = baseUrl + "&fieldCode=" + fieldCode;
        referralForm.popupWindow(url);
    } else {
        var message = "Please select the Recipient to access any Help information";
        var referrerTab = hlk.eref.Fn.getSummaryFrameDoc().getElementById("referrerDetailsTabTitle");
        if (referrerTab != null) {
            var tabTitle = $j(referrerTab).text();
            message = "Please select the Recipient (in " + jQuery.trim(tabTitle) + " tab) to access any Help information";
        }
        alert(message);
    }
}

function validatePatientDocuments(isSubmitEvent) {
    displayProcessingMessage();
    var hasSelectedWithEmptyContent = false;
    // reset and recalculate the total attachment content to be submitted to the Form server
    referralForm.attachmentContentSubmissionSize = 0;
    if (tabExists(hlk.eref.FormConstants.TAB_ATTACHMENTS)) {

        if (reportsTable != null) {
            var records = reportsTable.dt.getRecordSet().getRecords();
            for (var i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
                var id = records[i].getData("id");
                var contentHiddenFieldId = id + ".content";
                if (records[i].getData("selected") == "true") {
                    var name = records[i].getData("name");
                    var referenceId = records[i].getData("referenceID");
                    var conceptId = records[i].getData("conceptID");
                    var reportType = records[i].getData("reportType");
                    var mimeType = records[i].getData("type");
                    var content = reportsTable.getHiddenFieldValue(contentHiddenFieldId);
                    var patientDocument = null;
                    if (hlk.eref.PatientDocument.DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT_TYPE == reportType) {
                        patientDocument = referralForm.getReport(referenceId);
                    } else {
                        patientDocument = referralForm.getScannedDocument(referenceId);
                    }
                    if (patientDocument == null) {
                        patientDocument = new YAHOO.hlk.eref.PatientDocument(reportType,referenceId,conceptId);
                        if (hlk.eref.PatientDocument.DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT_TYPE == patientDocument.type) {
                            referralForm.addReport(patientDocument);
                        } else {
                            referralForm.addScannedDocument(patientDocument);
                        }
                    }
                    patientDocument.name = name;
                    patientDocument.mimeType = mimeType;

                    if (content == null && patientDocument.content == null) {
                        hasSelectedWithEmptyContent = true;
                    } else {
                        // If content from the jsp is not null, then set it to the cached patientDocument object
                        if (patientDocument.content == null) {
                            patientDocument.content = content;
                            patientDocument.size = content.length;
                            if (reportsTable.getHiddenFieldValue(id + ".size") == null) {
                                reportsTable.setHiddenField(id + ".size", content.length);
                            }
                        }// GREF-559: When RTF is not rendered in the view, we still need to post the content to the server so that 
                        // it pass the RTF validation to prevent it from being put into the Hospital File Reference table
                        else if ("application/rtf" == mimeType || referralForm.renderMimeTypesForPrintableView.get(mimeType) != null) {
                            // Sets the total attachment size so that we know when to display error. For more information, see EREF-2623
                            referralForm.attachmentContentSubmissionSize += patientDocument.content.length;
                            if (content == null) {
                                // if content from jsp is null and we need to render the content in printable view, then set the value so that it can be rendered in printable view/preview
                                reportsTable.setHiddenField(contentHiddenFieldId, patientDocument.content);
                                reportsTable.setHiddenField(id + ".size", patientDocument.content.length);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    // clear the content if not selected so that it won't be posted to the server
                    reportsTable.setHiddenField(contentHiddenFieldId, "");
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if (hasSelectedWithEmptyContent) {
        if (isSubmitEvent) {
            referralForm.retrievePatientDocsContent(handleRetrievePatientDocsContentOkForSubmit, handleRetrievePatientDocsContentErrorResponse);
        } else {
            referralForm.retrievePatientDocsContent(handleRetrievePatientDocsContentOkForPreview, handleRetrievePatientDocsContentErrorResponse);
        }
    } else {
        if (referralForm.isMaxMessageSizeExceeded()) {
            referralForm.isSubmitting = false;
            displayErrorMessage("The total uploaded files size exceeds maximum limit of " + referralForm.maxMessageSize + " . Please reduce files size and try again");
        } else {
            if (isSubmitEvent) {
                submitFormContinue();
            } else {
                previewFormContinue();
            }
        }
    }
}

function handleRetrievePatientDocsContentOkForPreview(responseElement) {
    if (handleRetrievePatientDocsContentOkResponse(responseElement) == true) {
        if (referralForm.isMaxMessageSizeExceeded()) {
            referralForm.isSubmitting = false;
            displayErrorMessage("The total uploaded files size exceeds maximum limit of " + referralForm.maxMessageSize + " . Please reduce files size and try again");
        } else {
            previewFormContinue();
        }
    } else {
        referralForm.isSubmitting = false;
    }
}

function handleRetrievePatientDocsContentOkForSubmit(responseElement) {
    if (handleRetrievePatientDocsContentOkResponse(responseElement) == true) {
        if (referralForm.isMaxMessageSizeExceeded()) {
            referralForm.isSubmitting = false;
            displayErrorMessage("The total uploaded files size exceeds maximum limit of " + referralForm.maxMessageSize + " . Please reduce files size and try again");
        } else {
            submitFormContinue();
        }
    }
}

function handleRetrievePatientDocsContentOkResponse(responseElement) {
    var errorOccurred = false;
    var errorMessage = "Please fix the following errors:<ul>";
    var records = reportsTable.dt.getRecordSet().getRecords();
    // Loop thru the reportsTable to make sure that all the selected rows got content/size
    for (var i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
        var selected = records[i].getData("selected");
        var reportType = records[i].getData("reportType");
        var name = records[i].getData("name");
        var id = records[i].getData("id");
        var referenceId = records[i].getData("referenceID");
        var mimeType = records[i].getData("type");
        if (selected == "true") {
            var patientDocument = null;
            if (hlk.eref.PatientDocument.DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT_TYPE == reportType) {
                patientDocument = referralForm.getReport(referenceId);
            } else {
                patientDocument = referralForm.getScannedDocument(referenceId);
            }
            if (patientDocument == null || patientDocument.content == null || patientDocument.content == "") {
                errorMessage += "<li class='errorList'>Unable to retrieve the content for the patient document. Report Type: " + reportType + ", Reference ID: " + referenceId + ", Name: " + name + ". Please fix the problem or remove it from the list to continue.";
                errorOccurred = true;
                reportsTable.highlightRowError(i);
            } else {
                // NOTE: We are setting directly to the hidden fields as we do not have a content field in the YUI recordset.
                // if we refresh the view at this stage, the content will be lost.
                reportsTable.setHiddenField(id + ".size", patientDocument.size);
                // GREF-559: When RTF is not rendered in the view, we still need to post the content to the server so that 
                // it pass the RTF validation to prevent it from being put into the Hospital File Reference table
                if ("application/rtf" == mimeType || referralForm.renderMimeTypesForPrintableView.get(mimeType) != null) {
                    // Sets the total attachment size so that we know when to display error. For more information, see EREF-2623
                    referralForm.attachmentContentSubmissionSize += patientDocument.content.length;
                    reportsTable.setHiddenField(id + ".content", patientDocument.content);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    errorMessage += "</ul>";

    if (errorOccurred == true) {
        hlk.eref.FormUtil.sendEventLog("formValidate", {
            "validate": "error",
            "errorMessage": errorMessage
        });
        hanldleDocumentContentValidationError(errorMessage);
        return false;
    } else {
        hlk.eref.FormUtil.sendEventLog("formValidate", {
            "validate": "success"
        });
        return true;
    }
}

function handleRetrievePatientDocsContentErrorResponse(errorText) {
    hlk.eref.FormUtil.sendEventLog("formValidate", {
        "validate": "error",
        "errorMessage": errorText
    });
    hanldleDocumentContentValidationError("Error occurred while retrieving the content of the patient documents: " + errorText);
}

function hanldleDocumentContentValidationError(message) {
    displayErrorMessage(message);
    referralForm.isSubmitting = false;
    tabManager.showErrorIndicator("attachmentsTabContainer");
}

function reloadForm() {
    displayFacilityReloadMessage();
    if (typeof ssiOnReloadForm == "function") {
        if (ssiOnReloadForm() == false) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    var referralForm = $("referralForm");
    var reloadEl = document.createElement("input");
    reloadEl.setAttribute("name", "reloadFacility");
    reloadEl.setAttribute("type", "submit");
    reloadEl.style.display = "none";
    referralForm.appendChild(reloadEl);
    reloadEl.click();
}

function submitForm() {

    if (referralForm.isOtherService) {
        showWarningForSubmitOtherService();
        return;
    }

    referralForm.isSubmitting = true;
    referralForm.isFromPreview = false;
    referralForm.isFromSubmit = true;

    validatePatientDocuments(true);
}

function submitFormContinue() {
    if (!submitted) {
        if (typeof setAccRecordSelected == "function") {
            setAccRecordSelected();
        }
        try {
            if (!beforeFormSubmit(true)) {
                referralForm.isSubmitting = false;
                displayErrorMessage("Submission is cancelled due to some validation errors pre-submission.");
                return;
            }
        } catch (e) {
            referralForm.isSubmitting = false;
            displayErrorMessage("Submission is cancelled due to some validation errors pre-submission: " + e.message);
            return;
        }
        submitted = true;
        // document.referralForm.submit();

        if (hlk.eref.FormUtil.isEventLogEnabled()) {
            // Collect comprehensive event log data
            var eventLogData = buildEventLogData();
            eventLogData.submit = "accepted";
            hlk.eref.FormUtil.doSendEventLog("form-submit-request", eventLogData);
        }
        $j("#referralForm").submit();
        displaySubmissionProgress();
    }
}

/**
 * Helper function to build comprehensive event log data
 * Replicates the data structure from referralFormContent.jsp
 */
function buildEventLogData() {
    // Helper function to safely get field value from DOM
    function getFieldValue(fieldId, useNativeMethod) {
        var element = useNativeMethod ? document.getElementByIdNative(fieldId) : document.getElementById(fieldId);
        return (element != null) ? element.value : "";
    }

    // Retrieve facility code
    var fCode = "";
    try {
        fCode = document.getElementById("administrative.facility").value;
    } catch (err) {
        fCode = "";
    }

    // Define field mappings for event log data collection
    // Format: [fieldId, useNativeMethod, eventLogPropertyName]
    var eventLogFields = [// Patient fields
    ["patientDob", true, "patientDob"], ["patientGender", true, "patientGender"], ["patient.indigenousStatus", false, "patientIndigenousStatus"], ["patientResidentialAddressState", true, "patientResidentialAddressState"], ["patientResidentialAddressPostCode", true, "patientResidentialAddressPostCode"], // Referrer fields
    ["serviceViewName", true, "serviceViewName"], ["referrerId", false, "medicareProviderNo"], ["referrer.id.hpii", false, "referrerIdHpii"], ["referrer.id.hpio", false, "referrerIdHpio"], ["referrerFirstName", true, "referrerFirstName"], ["referrerLastName", true, "referrerLastName"], ["referrerOrgName", true, "referrerOrgName"], ["practiceAddressLine1", true, "practiceAddressLine1"], ["practiceAddressLine2", true, "practiceAddressLine2"], ["practiceAddressSuburb", true, "practiceAddressSuburb"], ["practiceAddressState", true, "practiceAddressState"], ["practiceAddressPostCode", true, "practiceAddressPostCode"], ["referrerContactTelephone", true, "referrerContactTelephone"], ["referrerContactFax", true, "referrerContactFax"], ["referrerEmail", true, "referrerEmail"]];

    // Build event log data object dynamically
    var eventLogData = {
        "service": referralForm.serviceCode,
        "facilityCode": fCode,
        "referralId": referralForm.formScopeId,
        // EMR Interface information
        "emrInterfaceName": referralForm.emrInterfaceName,
        "emrInterfaceVersion": referralForm.emrInterfaceVersion,
        "formMethod": referralForm.formMethod,
        "emrApplicationName": getFieldValue("applicationName", false) || (typeof formModel !== "undefined" && formModel["hiso.pmsApplication"]) || "",
        "emrApplicationVersion": getFieldValue("applicationVersion", false) || (typeof formModel !== "undefined" && formModel["hiso.pmsApplicationVersion"]) || "",
        "portalMode": referralForm.isPortalMode(),
        "operationMode": getFieldValue("operationMode", false)
    };

    // Populate event log data from DOM fields
    for (var i = 0; i < eventLogFields.length; i++) {
        var fieldConfig = eventLogFields[i];
        eventLogData[fieldConfig[2]] = getFieldValue(fieldConfig[0], fieldConfig[1]);
    }

    return eventLogData;
}

function showWarningForSubmitOtherService() {
    if (!(referralForm.isOnPageLoad && referralForm.isFromPreview)) {
        hlk.util.Dialog.showWarning("'Other' is for non-DHB services and cannot be submitted as a DHB referral. You can use this referral form to print and fax to any non-DHB service. Use the 'Preview' function to open a copy of the referral and save locally (save as type: web page,HTML only).");
    }
}

function displaySubmissionProgress() {
    displayOkMessage("<span class='progress'>Submission in progress. Please wait</span>", false);
}

function beforeFormSubmit(isSubmitEvent) {

    if (isSubmitEvent) {
        onSubmitTabs();
    } else {
        onPreviewTabs();
    }

    if (typeof ssiOnSubmit == "function") {
        if (ssiOnSubmit(isSubmitEvent) == false) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;

}

function previewForm() {
    referralForm.isSubmitting = true;
    referralForm.isFromPreview = true;
    referralForm.isFromSubmit = false;
    validatePatientDocuments(false);
}

function previewFormContinue() {
    try {
        if (!beforeFormSubmit(false)) {
            referralForm.isSubmitting = false;
            displayErrorMessage("Preview is cancelled due to some validation errors.");
            return;
        }
    } catch (e) {
        referralForm.isSubmitting = false;
        displayErrorMessage("Preview is cancelled due to some validation errors: " + e.message);
        return;
    }

    displayProcessingMessage();
    var referralFormEl = $("referralForm");
    var previewEl = document.createElement("input");
    previewEl.setAttribute("name", "preview");
    previewEl.setAttribute("type", "submit");
    previewEl.style.display = "none";
    referralFormEl.appendChild(previewEl);
    previewEl.click();
}

function fetchPreview() {
    var printableViewForm = $("printableViewForm");

    var params = Form.serialize(printableViewForm);
    // a workaround for SmartSelect so that the label can be displayed in printable view
    var jSmartSelectLabel = jQuery(".SmartSelectElement input[type='hidden']").filter(function() {
        return this.value.length > 0
    });
    if (jSmartSelectLabel.length > 0) {
        params += "&" + jQuery.param(jSmartSelectLabel);
    }
    new Ajax.Request(printableViewForm.action,{
        method: 'post',
        parameters: params,
        onSuccess: fetchPreviewSuccess,
        onFailure: fetchPreviewFailure
    });
    return false;
}

function fetchPreviewSuccess(response) {
    try {
        var printableView = response.responseText;
        if (printableView != null) {
            var gPreviewWindow = popupWindow("");
            if (gPreviewWindow == null) {
                $j("#iframeDialog").dialog(hlk.eref.Fn.getIFrameDialogInitParams());
                hlk.util.DhtmlUtil.setIFrameContent(document, "iframeDialogFrame", printableView);
                $j("#iframeDialog").dialog("open");
            } else {
                gPreviewWindow.document.open();
                gPreviewWindow.document.write(printableView);
                gPreviewWindow.document.close();
                if (!gPreviewWindow.closed) {
                    gPreviewWindow.focus();
                }
            }
        } else {
            alert("preview response is null");
        }
    } catch (err) {
        alert("Preview error: " + err.description);
    }
}

function fetchPreviewFailure(response) {
    alert("Failed to generate preview: " + response.responseText);
}

function parkForm(isAutoSave) {
    if (referralForm.isFormScopeSessionTimeout && isAutoSave) {
        return;
    }
    //checking to see if the park button is disabled
    //this means the form cant be parked, which is the
    //equivalent of the autosave being disabled. so
    //dont allow saving.
    if (!referralForm.isParkButtonEnabled)
        return;

    referralForm.isParking = true;
    referralForm.isFromPreview = false;
    window.gAutoSave = isAutoSave;
    //using global variable window.gAutoSave as a word around of unable to pass parameters in the callback function
    parkFormWithParameters("referralForm", "park", "parkResult");
}

function parkFormWithParameters(formId, event, resultContainer) {
    if (window.gAutoSave != true) {
        displayProcessingMessage();
    }
    var form = document.getElementById(formId);
    var params = $j("#" + formId).serialize();
    if (event != null)
        params = event + '&' + params;

    new Ajax.Request(form.action,{
        method: 'post',
        postBody: params,
        onSuccess: pmsPark,
        onFailure: function(transport) {
            handleParkedErrorResponse("Error occurred when serializing form fields to xml: " + transport.responseText);
        }
    });
}

function pmsPark(transport) {
    try {
        var formXml = transport.responseText;
        //alert("index: " + formXml.indexOf("<referral")); 
        if (formXml.indexOf("<form") == 0) {
            var formRequestPath = document.hiddenForm.formRequestPath.value;
            var hisoMode = 'P';
            if (window.gAutoSave) {
                hisoMode = 'E';
            }

            // remove the patient document content before parking
            var formDoc = parseXmlDoc(formXml);
            // removes the content before saving/parking to EMR
            formDoc = hlk.eref.FormUtil.clearPatientDocumentContentValues(formDoc);
            formXml = toXmlString(formDoc);

            referralForm.parkFormToEmr(formXml, hisoMode, formRequestPath, handleParkedOkResponse, handleParkedErrorResponse);
        } else if (formXml.indexOf("Error") == 0 || formXml.indexOf("Your session to the HealthLink Forms Director has timed out") != -1) {
            referralForm.isFormScopeSessionTimeout = true;
            if (window.gAutoSave) {
                $('okMessageDiv').hide();
                // dont dispaly anything if it's auto-park and got session time out message
            } else {
                handleParkedErrorResponse(transport.responseText);
            }
        } else {
            handleParkedErrorResponse("Error occurred. Unexpected response from forms server: " + transport.responseText);
        }
    } finally {
        referralForm.isParking = false;
        hlk.eref.FormUtil.sendEventLog("form-park", {
            "park": "success",
            "referralId": referralForm.formScopeId
        });
    }
}

function handleParkedOkResponse(responseElement) {
    handleHisoParkedOkResponse(responseElement);
    referralForm.isParking = false;
}

function displayParkOKMessage() {
    if (window.gAutoSave) {
        displayOkMessage("Form has been auto-saved.", true, false);
    } else {
        displayOkMessage(hlk.eref.MessageConstants.PARK_SUCCESSFUL);
    }
}

function handleHisoParkedOkResponse(responseElement) {
    if (getElementLocalName(responseElement) == "saveContainerResponse") {
        var responseValue = selectSingleNodeValue(responseElement, "//ns:return/ns:response", {
            ns: referralForm.emr.FS_NS_URL
        });
        if (responseValue != null && responseValue.toLowerCase() == "true") {
            displayParkOKMessage();
        } else {
            if (window.gAutoSave) {
                hideProcessingMessage();
            } else {
                displayErrorMessage("Form not parked - The current system does not support parking of forms.");
            }
        }
    } else {
        handleParkedErrorResponse("Invalid Response: " + html2entities(responseElement.xml));
    }
}

function handleParkedErrorResponse(message) {
    if (window.gAutoSave) {
        displayErrorMessage("Failed to auto-save the form. " + message);
    } else {
        displayErrorMessage("Failed to park the form.<br/>" + message);
    }
    referralForm.isParking = false;
}

function displayErrorMessage(message) {
    $('okMessageDiv').hide();
    var d = $('errorMessageDiv');
    d.innerHTML = message;
    d.show();
    d.scrollIntoView();
}

function displayProcessingMessage() {
    displayOkMessage("<span class='progress'>Processing</span>", false);
}

function hideProcessingMessage() {
    $('okMessageDiv').hide();
}

function displayFacilityReloadMessage() {
    displayOkMessage("Processing facility change.", false);
}

function displayOkMessage(message, isCompleted, focusMessage) {
    $('errorMessageDiv').hide();
    var d = $('okMessageDiv');
    if (isCompleted == false) {
        d.addClassName("inProgress");
    } else {
        d.removeClassName("inProgress");
    }
    d.innerHTML = message;
    d.show();
    if (focusMessage != false) {
        d.scrollIntoView();
    }
}

function displayWarningMessage(message, focusMessage) {
    $('okMessageDiv').hide();
    var d = $('warningMessageDiv');
    d.innerHTML = message;
    d.show();
    if (focusMessage != false) {
        d.scrollIntoView();
    }
}

/* base64 encode/decode compatible with window.btoa/atob
 *
 * window.atob/btoa is a Firefox extension to convert binary data (the "b")
 * to base64 (ascii, the "a").
 *
 * It is also found in Safari and Chrome.  It is not available in IE.
 *
 * if (!window.btoa) window.btoa = base64.encode
 * if (!window.atob) window.atob = base64.decode
 *
 * The original spec's for atob/btoa are a bit lacking
 * https://developer.mozilla.org/en/DOM/window.atob
 * https://developer.mozilla.org/en/DOM/window.btoa
 *
 * window.btoa and base64.encode takes a string where charCodeAt is [0,255]
 * If any character is not [0,255], then an DOMException(5) is thrown.
 *
 * window.atob and base64.decode take a base64-encoded string
 * If the input length is not a multiple of 4, or contains invalid characters
 *   then an DOMException(5) is thrown.
 */
var base64 = {};
base64.PADCHAR = '=';
base64.ALPHA = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/';

base64.makeDOMException = function() {
    // sadly in FF,Safari,Chrome you can't make a DOMException
    var e, tmp;

    try {
        return new DOMException(DOMException.INVALID_CHARACTER_ERR);
    } catch (tmp) {
        // not available, just passback a duck-typed equiv
        // https://developer.mozilla.org/en/Core_JavaScript_1.5_Reference/Global_Objects/Error
        // https://developer.mozilla.org/en/Core_JavaScript_1.5_Reference/Global_Objects/Error/prototype
        var ex = new Error("DOM Exception 5");

        // ex.number and ex.description is IE-specific.
        ex.code = ex.number = 5;
        ex.name = ex.description = "INVALID_CHARACTER_ERR";

        // Safari/Chrome output format
        ex.toString = function() {
            return 'Error: ' + ex.name + ': ' + ex.message;
        }
        ;
        return ex;
    }
}

base64.getbyte64 = function(s, i) {
    // This is oddly fast, except on Chrome/V8.
    //  Minimal or no improvement in performance by using a
    //   object with properties mapping chars to value (eg. 'A': 0)
    var idx = base64.ALPHA.indexOf(s.charAt(i));
    if (idx === -1) {
        throw base64.makeDOMException();
    }
    return idx;
}

base64.decode = function(s) {
    // convert to string
    s = '' + s;
    var getbyte64 = base64.getbyte64;
    var pads, i, b10;
    var imax = s.length
    if (imax === 0) {
        return s;
    }

    if (imax % 4 !== 0) {
        throw base64.makeDOMException();
    }

    pads = 0
    if (s.charAt(imax - 1) === base64.PADCHAR) {
        pads = 1;
        if (s.charAt(imax - 2) === base64.PADCHAR) {
            pads = 2;
        }
        // either way, we want to ignore this last block
        imax -= 4;
    }

    var x = [];
    for (i = 0; i < imax; i += 4) {
        b10 = (getbyte64(s, i) << 18) | (getbyte64(s, i + 1) << 12) | (getbyte64(s, i + 2) << 6) | getbyte64(s, i + 3);
        x.push(String.fromCharCode(b10 >> 16, (b10 >> 8) & 0xff, b10 & 0xff));
    }

    switch (pads) {
    case 1:
        b10 = (getbyte64(s, i) << 18) | (getbyte64(s, i + 1) << 12) | (getbyte64(s, i + 2) << 6);
        x.push(String.fromCharCode(b10 >> 16, (b10 >> 8) & 0xff));
        break;
    case 2:
        b10 = (getbyte64(s, i) << 18) | (getbyte64(s, i + 1) << 12);
        x.push(String.fromCharCode(b10 >> 16));
        break;
    }
    return x.join('');
}

base64.getbyte = function(s, i) {
    var x = s.charCodeAt(i);
    if (x > 255) {
        throw base64.makeDOMException();
    }
    return x;
}

base64.encode = function(s) {
    if (arguments.length !== 1) {
        throw new SyntaxError("Not enough arguments");
    }
    var padchar = base64.PADCHAR;
    var alpha = base64.ALPHA;
    var getbyte = base64.getbyte;

    var i, b10;
    var x = [];

    // convert to string
    s = '' + s;

    var imax = s.length - s.length % 3;

    if (s.length === 0) {
        return s;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < imax; i += 3) {
        b10 = (getbyte(s, i) << 16) | (getbyte(s, i + 1) << 8) | getbyte(s, i + 2);
        x.push(alpha.charAt(b10 >> 18));
        x.push(alpha.charAt((b10 >> 12) & 0x3F));
        x.push(alpha.charAt((b10 >> 6) & 0x3f));
        x.push(alpha.charAt(b10 & 0x3f));
    }
    switch (s.length - imax) {
    case 1:
        b10 = getbyte(s, i) << 16;
        x.push(alpha.charAt(b10 >> 18) + alpha.charAt((b10 >> 12) & 0x3F) + padchar + padchar);
        break;
    case 2:
        b10 = (getbyte(s, i) << 16) | (getbyte(s, i + 1) << 8);
        x.push(alpha.charAt(b10 >> 18) + alpha.charAt((b10 >> 12) & 0x3F) + alpha.charAt((b10 >> 6) & 0x3f) + padchar);
        break;
    }
    return x.join('');
}

 * of this publication may be reproduced or transmitted in any form or by any
 * means, electronic or mechanical, including photocopying, recording, or any
 * information storage and retrieval system, without permission in writing from
 * the publisher. Infringers of copyright render themselves liable for
 * prosecution.
 * 
 * Dependency:	hlk/eref/Logger.js
 */

document.getElementByIdNative = document.getElementById;
document.getElementById = function(elemIdOrName) {
    var resultFoundById = false;
    var result = document.getElementByIdNative(elemIdOrName);
    if (result != null) {
        resultFoundById = true;
    }
    if (result == null) {
        var elems = document.getElementsByName(elemIdOrName);
        if (elems && elems.length > 0) {
            result = elems[0];
        }
    }
    if (result != null && !resultFoundById) {
        if (referralForm != null && referralForm.logger) {
            referralForm.logger.debug("getElementById", "getElementById: Element found by name instead of ID: " + elemIdOrName);
        }
    }

    return result;
}

/**
 * This defines the class for displaying an configuring Calendar.
 * It is using the YAHOO YUI implementation.
 * 
 * Dependencies: yui/calendar/calendar.js and its related files
 * YUI Version at implementation time: 2.7.0
 */

var hlk = hlk || {};
if (hlk.util == null || typeof (hlk.util) != "object") {
    hlk.util = new Object();
}

hlk.util.Calendar = {

    /**
	 * Construct a new YAHOO.widget.DateCellEditor with HLK customization
	 * @param createWithIframe whether to place an iframe shim underneath the Calendar to 
	 * prevent select elements from bleeding through
	 */
    constructDateCellEditor: function(createWithIframe) {
        var iframeVar = true;
        if (createWithIframe != null) {
            iframeVar = createWithIframe;
        }

        var navConfig = {
            strings: {
                month: "Choose Month",
                year: "Enter Year",
                submit: "OK",
                cancel: "Cancel",
                invalidYear: "Please enter a valid year"
            },
            monthFormat: YAHOO.widget.Calendar.SHORT,
            initialFocus: "year"
        };

        var calendarOptionsVar = {
            iframe: iframeVar,
            hide_blank_weeks: true,
            navigator: navConfig,
            DATE_FIELD_DELIMITER: "/",
            MDY_DAY_POSITION: 1,
            MDY_MONTH_POSITION: 2,
            MDY_YEAR_POSITION: 3,
            MD_DAY_POSITION: 1,
            MD_MONTH_POSITION: 2,
            MONTHS_SHORT: ["01", "02", "03", "04", "05", "06", "07", "08", "09", "10", "11", "12"]
        };

        var editor = new YAHOO.widget.DateCellEditor({
            calendarOptions: calendarOptionsVar
        });
        return editor;
    }
};
/*
 * Copyright (c) HealthLink Limited (2014).
 *
 * This document is copyrighted. Except for the purpose of fair reviewing, no part
 * of this publication may be reproduced or transmitted in any form or by any
 * means, electronic or mechanical, including photocopying, recording, or any
 * information storage and retrieval system, without permission in writing from
 * the publisher. Infringers of copyright render themselves liable for
 * prosecution.
 * 
 */

var hlk = hlk || {};
if (hlk.util == null || typeof (hlk.util) != "object") {
    hlk.util = new Object();
}

/**
 * Usage: in order to make a section expendable, firstly creating a section similar to the following in a JSP page
 * <div id="sectionRef">
 * 	 <div id="rowId_0" class="expandableRow"> <!-- the row ID can be any name with a _0 at the end -->
 *     <div class="expandableContent">
 *       // all section content
 *     </div>
 *     <div class="expandableControl">
 *       <div class="icon">
 *         <img src="images/delete_section.png" onclick="hlk.util.ExpandableList.deleteRow(this);" />
 *       </div>
 *       <div class="icon">
 *         <img src="images/add_section.png" onclick="hlk.util.ExpandableList.addRow();" />
 *       </div>
 *     </div>
 *   </div>
 * </div>  
 *       
 * To initialize the list, simply call hlk.util.ExpandableList.init(sectionRef, groupName, maxRows) in the pageLoad JavaScript
 * The last three arguments can be skipped as they have default value.
 * Image files can have names other than "add_section.png"/"delete_section.png", but if the add icon name is NOT "add.png", the fourth argument must be specified
 * In the above templete, the fourth argument should be "add_section.png". I.e. hlk.util.ExpandableList.init(sectionRef, groupName, maxRows, "add_section.png")
 * The parameter "groupName" is used to support IE7 only. If IE7 support is NOT required, leave it as an empty String, i.e. ""
 * Otherwise, it should bu a name of group from the validation file.
 * 
 */
hlk.util.ExpandableList = (function() {

    var sectionRef;
    // the expandable section ID
    var groupName;
    // the name of the group for repeat
    var maxRows;
    // the maximum number of repeatable components allowed in the expandable section
    var addIconName;
    // the name of the add row icon
    var errorClass;
    // the name of the error class used for validation
    var hiddenClass;
    // the name of the hidden class

    var init = function(sectionRefVal, groupNameVal, maxRowsVal, addIconNameVal, errorClassVal, hiddenClassVal) {
        sectionRef = sectionRefVal;
        groupName = groupNameVal;
        maxRows = maxRowsVal;
        addIconName = typeof (addIconNameVal) === "undefined" ? "add.png" : addIconNameVal;
        errorClass = typeof (errorClassVal) === "undefined" ? "error" : errorClassVal;
        hiddenClass = typeof (hiddenClassVal) === "undefined" ? "hidden" : hiddenClassVal;
    };

    var addRow = function() {
        var mySection = $j("#" + sectionRef);
        var myRows = mySection.find(".expandableRow");
        var rowTotal = myRows.length;

        // can't add more rows if there is no such row or it reaches maxRows
        if (rowTotal > 0 && rowTotal < maxRows) {
            var newRow = document.createElement("div");
            newRow.id = getRowIdPrefix(myRows[rowTotal - 1].id) + rowTotal;
            newRow.className = "expandableRow";
            newRow.innerHTML = myRows[rowTotal - 1].innerHTML;
            setComponentIndex(rowTotal, newRow);
            $j(newRow).find(":not(span)." + errorClass).removeClass(errorClass);
            $j(newRow).find("div.errorIcon").css("display", "");
            if ((" " + $j(newRow).find("div.errorIcon").attr("class") + " ").indexOf(" " + hiddenClass + " ") < 0) {
                $j(newRow).find("div.errorIcon").addClass(hiddenClass);
            }

            mySection.append(newRow);
            hlk.util.DhtmlUtil.clearChildValues(newRow.id);

            // remove old add icon
            myRows = mySection.find(".expandableRow");
            rowTotal = myRows.length;
            if (rowTotal > 1) {
                var controlsInPreviousRow = $j(myRows[rowTotal - 2]).find(".expandableControl img");
                for (var i = 0; i < controlsInPreviousRow.length; i++) {
                    if (controlsInPreviousRow[i].src.indexOf(addIconName) > -1) {
                        $(controlsInPreviousRow[i].parentElement).remove();
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return false;
    };

    var deleteRow = function(src) {
        var mySection = $j("#" + sectionRef);
        var myRows = mySection.find(".expandableRow");
        var rowTotal = myRows.length;

        if (rowTotal <= 1) {
            hlk.util.DhtmlUtil.clearChildValues(myRows[0].id);
        } else {
            var oldRow = $j(src).closest(".expandableRow");
            var oldRowIndex = getRowIndex(oldRow.attr("id"));

            oldRow.remove();
            if (oldRowIndex == rowTotal - 1) {
                // add "addRow" icon to 2nd last row if deleted the last row
                var lastRow = mySection.find(".expandableRow")[oldRowIndex - 1];
                $j(lastRow).find(".expandableControl").append(getAddRowControl());
            }

            resetRowsIndex(parseInt(oldRowIndex) + 1, myRows);
            resetComponentsIndex(parseInt(oldRowIndex) + 1, myRows);
        }

        return false;
    };

    var deleteAllRows = function() {
        return deleteRows(0);
    };

    var deleteAllAddedRows = function() {
        return deleteRows(1);
    };

    var getAddRowControl = function() {
        var newDiv = document.createElement("div");
        newDiv.className = "icon";
        newDiv.innerHTML = "<img src=\"images/" + addIconName + "\" onclick=\"hlk.util.ExpandableList.addRow();\" />";

        return newDiv;
    };

    var getRowIndex = function(rowId) {
        return rowId.slice((-1) * (rowId.length - rowId.lastIndexOf("_") - 1));
    };

    var getRowIdPrefix = function(rowId) {
        return rowId.slice(0, (-1) * (rowId.length - rowId.lastIndexOf("_") - 1));
    };

    var resetRowsIndex = function(startId, rows) {
        for (var i = startId; i < rows.length; i++) {
            rows[i].id = getRowIdPrefix(rows[i].id) + (i - 1);
        }
    };

    var setComponentIndex = function(id, row) {
        if ((/MSIE (\d+\.\d+);/.test(navigator.userAgent)) && new Number(RegExp.$1) < 8) {
            row.innerHTML = row.innerHTML.replace(new RegExp("(" + groupName + "\\[)\\d*(\\])","g"), "$1" + id + "$2");
        } else {
            $j(row).find("*").each(function() {
                $j.each(this.attributes, function() {
                    if (this.specified && this.value != "null") {
                        this.value = this.value.replace(/(.+?\[)\d*(\])/g, "$1" + id + "$2");
                    }
                });
            });
        }
    };

    var resetComponentsIndex = function(startId, rows) {
        for (var i = startId; i < rows.length; i++) {
            setComponentIndex(i - 1, rows[i]);
        }
    };

    var deleteRows = function(fromId) {
        var mySection = $j("#" + sectionRef);
        var myRows = mySection.find(".expandableRow");
        var rowTotal = myRows.length;

        for (var i = fromId; i < rowTotal; i++) {
            $j(myRows[i]).remove();
        }

        if (fromId > 0 && rowTotal > 1) {
            $j(myRows[fromId - 1]).find(".expandableControl").append(getAddRowControl());
        }

        return false;
    };

    return {
        init: init,
        addRow: addRow,
        deleteRow: deleteRow,
        deleteAllRows: deleteAllRows,
        deleteAllAddedRows: deleteAllAddedRows
    };

}
)();

/**
 * Dependency: jquery.js
 */
var gCalContainer;
var gCalendar;
var gTargetDateField;
var gTargetDateFieldImg;
var gCalendarIsModal = false;
var gCalendarOnAnotherModalDialog = false;
// TODO: Make this into an object

/**
 * Initialise the global calendar.  Need to call this at least once during page load.
 * @isModal set this to true if you want to show calendar on another model dialog
 */
function initCalendar(isModal) {
    if (isModal == true) {
        gCalendarIsModal = true;
    }
    // Lazy Dialog Creation - Wait to create the Dialog, and setup document click listeners, until the first time the button is clicked.

    if (!gCalContainer) {

        function resetHandler() {
            // Reset the current calendar page to the select date, or 
            // to today if nothing is selected.
            var selDates = gCalendar.getSelectedDates();
            var resetDate;

            if (selDates.length > 0) {
                resetDate = selDates[0];
            } else {
                resetDate = gCalendar.today;
            }

            gCalendar.cfg.setProperty("pagedate", gCalendar.cfg.getProperty("MONTHS_SHORT")[resetDate.getMonth()] + "/" + resetDate.getFullYear());
            gCalendar.render();
        }

        function todayHandler() {
            var todayDate = gCalendar.today;

            gCalendar.cfg.setProperty("pagedate", gCalendar.cfg.getProperty("MONTHS_SHORT")[todayDate.getMonth()] + "/" + todayDate.getFullYear());
            //gCalendar.render();
            var month = todayDate.getMonth() + 1;
            if (month < 10) {
                month = '0' + month;
            }
            var day = todayDate.getDate();
            if (day < 10) {
                day = '0' + day;
            }
            finishDateSelection(todayDate.getFullYear(), month, day);
        }

        function closeHandler() {
            gCalContainer.hide();
            gTargetDateField.focus();
        }

        gCalContainer = new YAHOO.widget.Dialog("calContainer",{
            visible: false,
            buttons: [{
                text: "Reset",
                handler: resetHandler,
                isDefault: true
            }, {
                text: "Today",
                handler: todayHandler
            }, {
                text: "Close",
                handler: closeHandler
            }],
            draggable: false,
            modal: gCalendarIsModal,
            // setting this to true causes null exception when showing in another modal dialog. Set showOnAnotherModalDialog=true in showCal() to workaround this problem until it is fixed (upgrade YUI?)
            close: true
        });
        gCalContainer.setHeader('Select Date');
        gCalContainer.setBody('<div id="cal"></div>');
        gCalContainer.render(document.body);

        gCalContainer.showEvent.subscribe(function() {
            if (YAHOO.env.ua.ie) {
                // Since we're hiding the table using yui-overlay-hidden, we 
                // want to let the gCalContainer know that the content size has changed, when
                // shown
                gCalContainer.fireEvent("changeContent");
            }
        });
    }

    // Lazy gCalendar Creation - Wait to create the Calendar until the first time the button is clicked.
    if (!gCalendar) {

        var navConfig = {
            strings: {
                month: "Choose Month",
                year: "Enter Year",
                submit: "OK",
                cancel: "Cancel",
                invalidYear: "Please enter a valid year"
            },
            monthFormat: YAHOO.widget.Calendar.SHORT,
            initialFocus: "year"
        };

        gCalendar = new YAHOO.widget.Calendar("cal",{
            iframe: false,
            // Turn iframe off, since container has iframe support.
            hide_blank_weeks: true,
            // Enable, to demonstrate how we handle changing height, using changeContent
            navigator: navConfig,
            DATE_FIELD_DELIMITER: "/",
            MDY_DAY_POSITION: 1,
            MDY_MONTH_POSITION: 2,
            MDY_YEAR_POSITION: 3,
            MD_DAY_POSITION: 1,
            MD_MONTH_POSITION: 2,
            MONTHS_SHORT: ["01", "02", "03", "04", "05", "06", "07", "08", "09", "10", "11", "12"]
        });
        gCalendar.render();
        gCalendar.renderEvent.subscribe(function() {
            // Tell Dialog it's contents have changed, which allows 
            // container to redraw the underlay (for IE6/Safari2)
            gCalContainer.fireEvent("changeContent");
        });
    }
}

/**
 * Shows the calendar.
 * @param targetId the target textbox where the date is to be enterted into
 * @param showOnAnotherModalDialog Set this to true when using this calendar in another modal dialog.
 */
function showCal(targetId, showOnAnotherModalDialog) {
    if (showOnAnotherModalDialog == true) {
        gCalendarOnAnotherModalDialog = true;
    } else {
        gCalendarOnAnotherModalDialog = false;
    }
    gTargetDateField = YAHOO.util.Dom.get(targetId);
    gTargetDateFieldImg = YAHOO.util.Dom.get(targetId + ".img");

    gCalContainer.cfg.setProperty("context", [targetId, "tl", "bl"]);

    // Remove select listener before we programatically select it
    gCalendar.selectEvent.unsubscribe(handleDateSelect);

    var seldate = gTargetDateField.value;
    var valid = validateDateFieldPattern(seldate);
    if (!valid) {
        //alert("The date in the field is not in valid format: dd/mm/yyyy. Calendar will show today as the initial date.");
        if (gCalendar != null) {
            seldate = gCalendar.today.getDate() + "/" + (gCalendar.today.getMonth() + 1) + "/" + gCalendar.today.getFullYear();
        }
    }
    if (seldate.length > 0) {
        gCalendar.select(seldate);
        var selDateObj = gCalendar.getSelectedDates()[0];
        // Set the pagedate to show the selected date if it exists
        gCalendar.cfg.setProperty("pagedate", gCalendar.cfg.getProperty("MONTHS_SHORT")[selDateObj.getMonth()] + "/" + selDateObj.getFullYear());
        gCalendar.render();
    }

    // Add select listener
    gCalendar.selectEvent.subscribe(handleDateSelect);

    gCalContainer.hideEvent.unsubscribe(handleHideEvent);
    gCalContainer.hideEvent.subscribe(handleHideEvent);

    // Hide Calendar if we click anywhere in the document other than the calendar
    YAHOO.util.Event.removeListener(document, "click", handleClick);
    YAHOO.util.Event.addListener(document, "click", handleClick);

    if (gCalendarIsModal && gCalendarOnAnotherModalDialog == false) {
        // Call mask if it's not on another modal dialog so that the parent mask is turned on
        var parentWindow = window.parent;
        for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
            if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].mask == "function") {
                parentWindow.frames[i].mask();
            }
        }
    }

    if (gCalendarIsModal == true) {
        // TODO: YUI is throwing exception when showing as modal.
        // Upgrade YUI to fix this. This is only temporary solution for now and should only be used with caution. 
        try {
            gCalContainer.show();
        } catch (e) {}
    } else {
        gCalContainer.show();
    }
}

/* check if the date value is in valid format: dd/mm/yyyy. 
 * return true if it's valid format. 
 */
function validateDateFieldPattern(dateValue) {
    var pattern = /\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}/
    if (pattern.test(dateValue)) {
        return true;
    } else {
        return false;
    }

}

function handleHideEvent(e) {
    gCalendar.selectEvent.unsubscribe(handleDateSelect);
    // remove the listener once a calendar is hidden
    YAHOO.util.Event.removeListener(document, "click", handleClick);
    gCalContainer.hideEvent.unsubscribe(handleHideEvent);

    if (gCalendarIsModal && gCalendarOnAnotherModalDialog == false) {
        // Only call unmask if it's not on another modal dialog so that the parent mask is removed
        var parentWindow = window.parent;
        for (var i = 0; i < parentWindow.frames.length; i++) {
            if (typeof parentWindow.frames[i].unmask == "function") {
                parentWindow.frames[i].unmask();
            }
        }
    }
    gTargetDateField.focus();
}

function handleDateSelect(type, args) {
    var dates = args[0];
    var date = dates[0];
    var year = new String(date[0])
      , month = new String(date[1])
      , day = new String(date[2]);
    if (month.length < 2) {
        month = "0".concat(month);
    }
    if (day.length < 2) {
        day = "0".concat(day);
    }
    finishDateSelection(year, month, day);
}

function finishDateSelection(year, month, day) {
    gTargetDateField.value = day + "/" + month + "/" + year;

    // WORKS WITH IE AND FIREFOX
    $j(gTargetDateField).change();

    gCalContainer.hide();
    gTargetDateField.focus();
}

function handleClick(e) {
    var el = YAHOO.util.Event.getTarget(e);
    var calContainerEl = gCalContainer.element;
    if (el != calContainerEl && !YAHOO.util.Dom.isAncestor(calContainerEl, el) && el != gTargetDateFieldImg && !YAHOO.util.Dom.isAncestor(gTargetDateFieldImg, el)) {
        gCalContainer.hide();
        gTargetDateField.focus();
    }
}
